# GYM2 - UI design + Liquid Glass (Jetpack Compose, Android)
# Each file is delimited by ===== FILE: <path> =====
# Required dependency for glass: io.github.kyant0:backdrop
# Assets not included (binary): res/drawable/img_liquid_glass_bg.jpg, fonts res/font/vazirmatn*.ttf

===== FILE: README.md =====
UI layers of the GYM2 app: theme/ (Color, Theme, Type), glass/ (GlassChrome, GlassTilt), components/CommonUi.kt, navigation/NavRoutes.kt, screens/*, MainActivity.kt (scaffold), and res/values theme+color XMLs.
ViewModels/data layer are omitted.

===== FILE: java/com/example/ui/theme/Color.kt =====

// --- 8 Accent Palettes Specification ---
data class LiquidPalette(
    val id: String,
    val persianName: String,
    val dayAccent: Color,
    val nightAccent: Color,
    val gradientStart: Color,
    val gradientEnd: Color
) {
    fun getAccent(isDark: Boolean): Color = if (isDark) nightAccent else dayAccent

    fun getCardGradient(): Brush = Brush.linearGradient(
        colors = listOf(gradientStart, gradientEnd)
    )
}

object LiquidPalettes {
    val SPORT_RED = LiquidPalette(
        id = "SPORT_RED",
        persianName = "اسپورت قرمز و مشکی",
        dayAccent = Color(0xFFFF6682),
        nightAccent = Color(0xFFFF3355),
        gradientStart = Color(0xFFE5123F),
        gradientEnd = Color(0xFF14060A)

    val GOLD_CARBON = LiquidPalette(

        persianName = "طلایی و کربن",
        dayAccent = Color(0xFFFFCA45),
        nightAccent = Color(0xFFFFB020),

        gradientEnd = Color(0xFF1A1203)

    val OCEAN = LiquidPalette(

        persianName = "اقیانوس آبی",
        dayAccent = Color(0xFF66B8FF),
        nightAccent = Color(0xFF3B9BFF),
        gradientStart = Color(0xFF1E90FF),
        gradientEnd = Color(0xFF021B3A)
    )

    val EMERALD = LiquidPalette(
        id = "EMERALD",
        persianName = "زمردی ورزشی",
        dayAccent = Color(0xFF47E89E),
        nightAccent = Color(0xFF34D17F),
        gradientStart = Color(0xFF19C37D),
        gradientEnd = Color(0xFF03281A)
    )

    val BRONZE_MEDAL = LiquidPalette(
        id = "BRONZE_MEDAL",
        persianName = "برنز قهرمانی",
        dayAccent = Color(0xFFFFAA5C),
        nightAccent = Color(0xFFE88635),
        gradientStart = Color(0xFFC8641C),
        gradientEnd = Color(0xFF1C0E04)
    )

    val VIOLET = LiquidPalette(
        id = "VIOLET",
        persianName = "بنفش نئون",
        dayAccent = Color(0xFFC2A8FF),
        nightAccent = Color(0xFFA78BFA),
        gradientStart = Color(0xFF8B5CF6),
        gradientEnd = Color(0xFF1E1250)
    )

    val ROSE = LiquidPalette(
        id = "ROSE",
        persianName = "رز پینک",
        dayAccent = Color(0xFFFF75BA),
        nightAccent = Color(0xFFFF4D9D),
        gradientStart = Color(0xFFFF4D9D),
        gradientEnd = Color(0xFF3A0A2A)
    )

    val PLATINUM_STEEL = LiquidPalette(
        id = "PLATINUM_STEEL",
        persianName = "تیتانیوم نقره‌ای",
        dayAccent = Color(0xFFC4D0E0),
        nightAccent = Color(0xFF9FB0C7),
        gradientStart = Color(0xFF5D6F85),
        gradientEnd = Color(0xFF10151D)
    )

    val all = listOf(
        SPORT_RED,
        GOLD_CARBON,
        OCEAN,
        EMERALD,
        BRONZE_MEDAL,
        VIOLET,
        ROSE,
        PLATINUM_STEEL
    )

    fun fromId(id: String): LiquidPalette {
        return all.firstOrNull { it.id.equals(id, ignoreCase = true) }
            ?: when (id.uppercase()) {
                "RED" -> SPORT_RED
                "DARK" -> SPORT_RED
                "LIGHT" -> SPORT_RED
                "SYSTEM" -> SPORT_RED
                "PINK" -> ROSE
                "PURPLE" -> VIOLET
                "SUNSET" -> BRONZE_MEDAL
                "CYBER" -> PLATINUM_STEEL
                else -> SPORT_RED
            }
    }
}

package com.example.ui.theme

import android.app.Activity
import androidx.compose.foundation.isSystemInDarkTheme

import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material3.LocalContentColor
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Shapes
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.lightColorScheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.CompositionLocalProvider
import androidx.compose.runtime.ReadOnlyComposable
import androidx.compose.runtime.SideEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.compositionLocalOf
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Brush
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.compositeOver
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.luminance
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.toArgb
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalView
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
import com.example.ui.glass.Glass
import com.example.ui.glass.GlassLook

import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min

/**
 * رنگ متن/آیکون خوانا روی یک پس‌زمینه: سفید یا مشکی، هرکدام کنتراست بیشتری دارد.
 */
fun onColorFor(background: Color): Color {
    val l = background.luminance()
    val whiteContrast = 1.05f / (l + 0.05f)
    val darkContrast = (l + 0.05f) / 0.0556f
    return if (whiteContrast >= darkContrast) Color.White else Color(0xFF111113)
}

/**
 * نسبت کنتراست WCAG 2.1 بین رنگ رویی (با پشتیبانی از آلفا) و رنگ پس‌زمینه.
 */
fun wcagContrastRatio(foreground: Color, background: Color): Float {
    val opaqueBg = background.copy(alpha = 1f)
    val effectiveFg = if (foreground.alpha < 1f) foreground.compositeOver(opaqueBg) else foreground
    val l1 = effectiveFg.luminance()
    val l2 = opaqueBg.luminance()
    val brighter = max(l1, l2)
    val darker = min(l1, l2)
    return (brighter + 0.05f) / (darker + 0.05f)
}

fun liquidGlassSurfaceColor(palette: LiquidPalette, isDark: Boolean): Color {
    return if (isDark) {
        androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF0B0F16), palette.gradientEnd, 0.32f)
    } else {
        androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF121A26), palette.gradientEnd, 0.28f)
    }
}

fun liquidSurfaceSolidColor(palette: LiquidPalette, isDark: Boolean): Color {
    return if (isDark) {
        androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF141A22), palette.gradientEnd, 0.30f).copy(alpha = 0.80f)
    } else {
        androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF182230), palette.gradientEnd, 0.26f).copy(alpha = 0.78f)
    }
}

fun liquidSurfaceElevatedColor(palette: LiquidPalette, isDark: Boolean): Color {
    return if (isDark) {
        androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF1E2530), palette.nightAccent, 0.18f).copy(alpha = 0.86f)
    } else {
        androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF1E2A3A), palette.dayAccent, 0.20f).copy(alpha = 0.72f)
    }
}

fun liquidBackgroundNeutralColor(palette: LiquidPalette, isDark: Boolean): Color {
    return if (isDark) {
        androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF06080B), palette.gradientEnd, 0.25f)
    } else {
        androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF13222E), palette.gradientStart, 0.26f)
    }
}

fun liquidTextPrimaryColor(isDark: Boolean): Color {
    return DarkTextPrimary
}

fun liquidTextSecondaryColor(palette: LiquidPalette, isDark: Boolean): Color {
    return if (isDark) {
        Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.82f)
    } else {
        Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.88f)
    }
}

/** چرخاندن رنگ‌مایه (hue) برای ساخت طیف هم‌خانواده از رنگ اصلی پالت. */
fun Color.shiftHue(degrees: Float, satMul: Float = 1f, valMul: Float = 1f): Color {
    val hsv = FloatArray(3)
    android.graphics.Color.colorToHSV(this.toArgb(), hsv)
    hsv[0] = ((hsv[0] + degrees) % 360f + 360f) % 360f
    hsv[1] = (hsv[1] * satMul).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    hsv[2] = (hsv[2] * valMul).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    return Color(android.graphics.Color.HSVToColor(hsv))
}

val LocalLiquidPalette = compositionLocalOf { LiquidPalettes.SPORT_RED }
val LocalIsDarkTheme = compositionLocalOf { true }

object LiquidTheme {
    val palette: LiquidPalette
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = LocalLiquidPalette.current

    val isDark: Boolean
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = LocalIsDarkTheme.current

    val glassColors
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = Glass.colors

    val accentColor: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = Glass.colors.tint

    // رنگ آیکون‌ها و برجسته‌سازی‌ها: همگی از پالت انتخابی کاربر ساخته می‌شوند
    val iconRed: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = accentColor
    val iconOrange: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = accentColor.shiftHue(24f)
    val iconGreen: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = accentColor.shiftHue(-24f, valMul = 1.05f)
    val iconBlue: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = accentColor.shiftHue(48f, 0.92f)
    val iconCyan: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = accentColor.shiftHue(-48f, 0.9f)
    val iconPurple: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = accentColor.shiftHue(72f, 0.85f)
    val iconIndigo: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = accentColor.shiftHue(-72f, 0.85f)

    val onAccent: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = onColorFor(accentColor)

    val cardGradient: Brush
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = palette.getCardGradient()

    /**
     * رنگ پایه کپسول‌ها و سطوح شیشه‌ای بدون نور سفید خیره‌کننده در روز
     * و با عمق رنگی هماهنگ با پالت در شب و پس‌زمینه انتخابی کاربر.
     */
    val glassSurfaceColor: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = Glass.colors.cell

    val surfaceSolid: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = Glass.colors.solidCell

    val surfaceElevated: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = Glass.colors.elevatedCell

    val backgroundNeutral: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = if (Glass.colors.wallpaper == GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL) {
            liquidBackgroundNeutralColor(palette, isDark)
        } else {
            GlassPalette.wallpaperTop(Glass.colors.wallpaper, palette, isDark)
        }

    val dividerColor: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = Glass.colors.separator

    val textPrimary: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = Glass.colors.label

    val textSecondary: Color
        @Composable
        @ReadOnlyComposable
        get() = Glass.colors.label2
}

fun createLiquidColorScheme(palette: LiquidPalette, isDark: Boolean): ColorScheme {
    val accent = palette.getAccent(isDark)
    val onAccent = onColorFor(accent)
    val bg = liquidBackgroundNeutralColor(palette, isDark)
    return if (isDark) {
        val card = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF14181F), palette.gradientEnd, 0.26f)
        val elevated = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFF1E242E), palette.gradientEnd, 0.30f)
        darkColorScheme(
            primary = accent,
            onPrimary = onAccent,
            primaryContainer = accent.copy(alpha = 0.20f),
            onPrimaryContainer = accent,
            secondary = accent,
            onSecondary = onAccent,
            background = bg,
            onBackground = DarkTextPrimary,
            surface = card,
            onSurface = DarkTextPrimary,
            surfaceVariant = elevated,
            onSurfaceVariant = DarkTextSecondary,
            outline = DarkDivider,
            error = SemanticError,
            onError = Color.White
        )
    } else {
        val card = liquidGlassSurfaceColor(palette, isDark = false)
        val elevated = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(Color(0xFFDCE4EE), palette.gradientStart, 0.16f)
        val inkPrimary = liquidTextPrimaryColor(isDark = false)
        val inkSecondary = liquidTextSecondaryColor(palette, isDark = false)
        lightColorScheme(
            primary = accent,
            onPrimary = onAccent,
            primaryContainer = accent.copy(alpha = 0.16f),
            onPrimaryContainer = accent,
            secondary = accent,
            onSecondary = onAccent,
            background = bg,
            onBackground = inkPrimary,
            surface = card,
            onSurface = inkPrimary,
            surfaceVariant = elevated,
            onSurfaceVariant = inkSecondary,
            outline = LightDivider,
            error = SemanticError,
            onError = Color.White
        )
    }
}

// Backward compatibility helper
fun getThemeColorScheme(themeName: String, systemIsDark: Boolean): ColorScheme {
    val isDark = when (themeName.uppercase()) {
        "LIGHT" -> false
        "DARK" -> true
        "SYSTEM" -> systemIsDark
        else -> systemIsDark
    }
    val palette = LiquidPalettes.fromId(themeName)
    return createLiquidColorScheme(palette, isDark)
}

@Composable
fun GymTheme(
    appearanceMode: String = "SYSTEM",
    accentPalette: String = "SPORT_RED",
    glassMaterial: String = "CLEAR",
    glassWallpaper: String = "BOTANICAL",
    glassLists: Boolean = true,
    shrinkTabBar: Boolean = true,
    motionHighlights: Boolean = false,
    reduceTransparency: Boolean = false,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val systemIsDark = isSystemInDarkTheme()
    val isDark = when (appearanceMode.uppercase()) {
        "LIGHT" -> false
        "DARK" -> true
        else -> systemIsDark
    }

    val palette = LiquidPalettes.fromId(accentPalette)
    val resolvedMaterial = GlassMaterial.fromId(glassMaterial)
    val resolvedWallpaper = GlassWallpaper.fromId(glassWallpaper)
    val glassColors = androidx.compose.runtime.remember(
        isDark,
        palette,
        resolvedMaterial,
        resolvedWallpaper,
        glassLists,
        reduceTransparency
    ) {
        GlassPalette.of(
            dark = isDark,
            palette = palette,
            material = resolvedMaterial,
            wallpaper = resolvedWallpaper,
            glassLists = glassLists,
            reduceTransparency = reduceTransparency
        )
    }
    val glassLook = androidx.compose.runtime.remember(
        glassColors,
        resolvedMaterial,
        resolvedWallpaper,
        glassLists,
        shrinkTabBar,
        motionHighlights,
        reduceTransparency
    ) {
        GlassLook(
            colors = glassColors,
            material = resolvedMaterial,
            wallpaper = resolvedWallpaper,
            glassLists = glassLists,
            shrinkTabBar = shrinkTabBar,
            motionHighlights = motionHighlights,
            reduceTransparency = reduceTransparency
        )
    }

    val baseScheme = createLiquidColorScheme(palette, isDark)
    val colorScheme = if (resolvedWallpaper == GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL) {
        baseScheme
    } else if (isDark) {
        baseScheme.copy(
            onBackground = glassColors.label,
            surface = glassColors.solidCell,
            onSurface = glassColors.label,
            surfaceVariant = glassColors.elevatedCell,
            onSurfaceVariant = glassColors.label2,
            outline = glassColors.separator
        )
    } else {
        baseScheme.copy(
            background = GlassPalette.wallpaperTop(resolvedWallpaper, palette, dark = false),
            onBackground = glassColors.label,
            surface = glassColors.solidCell,
            onSurface = glassColors.label,
            surfaceVariant = glassColors.elevatedCell,
            onSurfaceVariant = glassColors.label2,
            outline = glassColors.separator
        )
    }
    val defaultInk = glassColors.label

    val view = LocalView.current
    if (!view.isInEditMode) {
        SideEffect {
            val window = (view.context as? Activity)?.window ?: return@SideEffect
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            window.statusBarColor = Color.Transparent.toArgb()
            @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
            window.navigationBarColor = Color.Transparent.toArgb()
            val insetsController = WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, view)
            val useDarkSystemIcons = !glassColors.darkBackdrop
            insetsController.isAppearanceLightStatusBars = useDarkSystemIcons
            insetsController.isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = useDarkSystemIcons
        }
    }

    CompositionLocalProvider(
        LocalLiquidPalette provides palette,
        LocalIsDarkTheme provides isDark,
        LocalGlassLook provides glassLook
    ) {
        MaterialTheme(
            colorScheme = colorScheme,
            typography = AppTypography,
            shapes = Shapes(extraSmall = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
        ) {
            CompositionLocalProvider(LocalContentColor provides defaultInk) {
                content()
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: java/com/example/ui/glass/GlassChrome.kt =====
package com.example.ui.glass

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.os.Build
import androidx.compose.animation.animateColorAsState
import androidx.compose.animation.core.Spring
import androidx.compose.animation.core.animateFloatAsState
import androidx.compose.animation.core.spring
import androidx.compose.animation.core.tween
import androidx.compose.foundation.Image

enum class GlassMaterial(val id: String, val persianName: String) {
    CLEAR("CLEAR", "شفاف (شیشه‌ای)"),
    TINTED("TINTED", "مات و خوانا");

    companion object {
        fun fromId(id: String): GlassMaterial =
            entries.firstOrNull { it.id.equals(id, ignoreCase = true) } ?: CLEAR
    }
}

enum class GlassWallpaper(val id: String, val persianName: String) {
    BOTANICAL("BOTANICAL", "جنگل استوایی"),
    MIST("MIST", "مه نقره‌ای"),
    LAGOON("LAGOON", "تالاب آبی"),
    DUSK("DUSK", "شفق گرگ‌ومیش"),
    GRAPHITE("GRAPHITE", "گرافیت مدرن"),
    PLAIN("PLAIN", "ساده و مینیمال");

    fun isDarkBackdrop(systemDark: Boolean): Boolean = when (this) {
        BOTANICAL -> true
        else -> systemDark
    }

    companion object {
        fun fromId(id: String): GlassWallpaper =
            entries.firstOrNull { it.id.equals(id, ignoreCase = true) } ?: BOTANICAL
    }
}

fun isGlassRenderEffectSupported(): Boolean = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S
fun isGlassLensSupported(): Boolean = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU

object GlassShapes {
    val cell = androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape(24.dp)
    val module = androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)
    val capsule = androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape(percent = 50)
    val badge = androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)
}

@Immutable
data class GlassColors(
    val dark: Boolean,
    val darkBackdrop: Boolean,
    val tint: Color,
    val label: Color,
    val label2: Color,
    val label3: Color,
    val separator: Color,
    val fill: Color,
    val fill2: Color,
    val cell: Color,
    val solidCell: Color,
    val elevatedCell: Color,
    val chrome: Color,
    val chromeSolid: Color,
    val module: Color,
    val rimHi: Color,
    val rimMid: Color,
    val rimLo: Color,
    val highlight: Color,
    val platter: Color,
    val thumb: Color,
    val shadow: Color,
    val translucent: Boolean,
    val reduced: Boolean,
    val wallpaper: GlassWallpaper,
    val material: GlassMaterial,
)

object GlassPalette {
    fun of(
        dark: Boolean,
        palette: LiquidPalette,
        material: GlassMaterial,
        wallpaper: GlassWallpaper,
        glassLists: Boolean,
        reduceTransparency: Boolean,
    ): GlassColors {
        val darkBackdrop = wallpaper.isDarkBackdrop(dark)
        val tinted = material == GlassMaterial.TINTED
        val isBotanical = wallpaper == GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL
        val rawAccent = palette.getAccent(dark)
        val accent = if (darkBackdrop) {
            rawAccent
        } else {
            val lFg = (0.2126f * rawAccent.red + 0.7152f * rawAccent.green + 0.0722f * rawAccent.blue)
            val lBg = 0.90f // light frosted glass card luminance
            val contrast = (lBg + 0.05f) / (lFg + 0.05f)
            if (contrast >= 3.2f) {
                rawAccent
            } else {
                val baseStart = palette.gradientStart
                val startLum = 0.2126f * baseStart.red + 0.7152f * baseStart.green + 0.0722f * baseStart.blue
                if ((lBg + 0.05f) / (startLum + 0.05f) >= 3.4f) {
                    baseStart
                } else {
                    lerp(baseStart, Color(0xFF101520), 0.35f)
                }
            }
        }

        val baseGlass = liquidGlassSurfaceColor(palette, dark)
        val baseSolid = liquidSurfaceSolidColor(palette, dark)
        val baseElevated = liquidSurfaceElevatedColor(palette, dark)

        val solidCell = when {
            isBotanical -> baseSolid.copy(alpha = 0.94f)
            dark -> lerp(Color(0xFF1C1C1E), palette.gradientEnd, 0.14f)
            else -> Color.White
        }

        val module = when {
            reduceTransparency -> solidCell
            isBotanical && dark && tinted -> lerp(baseGlass, Color(0xFF0E131B), 0.2f).copy(alpha = 0.82f)
            isBotanical && dark -> baseGlass.copy(alpha = 0.40f)
            isBotanical && tinted -> baseGlass.copy(alpha = 0.80f)
            isBotanical -> baseGlass.copy(alpha = 0.42f)
            dark && tinted -> lerp(Color(0xFF28282C), palette.gradientEnd, 0.14f).copy(alpha = 0.86f)
            dark -> lerp(Color(0xFF2C2C30), palette.gradientEnd, 0.12f).copy(alpha = 0.42f)
            tinted -> lerp(Color.White, palette.gradientStart, 0.04f).copy(alpha = 0.86f)
            else -> lerp(Color.White, palette.gradientStart, 0.03f).copy(alpha = 0.54f)
        }

        val chromeSolid = when {
            isBotanical -> baseSolid.copy(alpha = 0.96f)
            dark -> Color(0xFF2C2C2E)
            else -> Color(0xFFF9F9FB)
        }

        val chrome = when {
            reduceTransparency -> chromeSolid
            isBotanical && dark && tinted -> baseGlass.copy(alpha = 0.84f)
            isBotanical && dark -> baseGlass.copy(alpha = 0.44f)
            isBotanical && tinted -> baseGlass.copy(alpha = 0.82f)
            isBotanical -> baseGlass.copy(alpha = 0.48f)
            dark && tinted -> Color(0xDB242426)
            dark -> Color(0x661E1E20)
            tinted -> Color(0xCCFFFFFF)
            else -> Color(0x68FFFFFF)
        }

        val label = if (darkBackdrop) Color.White else Color(0xFF0F131A)
        val label2 = when {
            isBotanical && !dark -> Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.88f)
            darkBackdrop -> Color(0xCCEBEBF5)
            else -> Color(0xB83C3C43)
        }
        val label3 = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x5CEBEBF5) else Color(0x523C3C43)
        val separator = if (darkBackdrop) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.16f) else Color(0x2E3C3C43)

        val elevated = when {
            isBotanical -> baseElevated
            dark -> Color(0xFF2C2C30)
            else -> Color(0xFFE5E5EA)
        }

        return GlassColors(
            dark = dark,
            darkBackdrop = darkBackdrop,
            tint = accent,
            label = label,
            label2 = label2,
            label3 = label3,
            separator = separator,
            fill = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x5C787880) else Color(0x33787880),
            fill2 = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x3D767680) else Color(0x1F767680),
            cell = if (glassLists) module else solidCell,
            solidCell = solidCell,
            elevatedCell = elevated,
            chrome = chrome,
            chromeSolid = chromeSolid,
            module = module,
            rimHi = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0xADFFFFFF) else Color.White,
            rimMid = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x24FFFFFF) else Color(0x5CFFFFFF),
            rimLo = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x47FFFFFF) else Color(0xCCFFFFFF),
            highlight = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x42FFFFFF) else Color(0xF5FFFFFF),
            platter = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x24FFFFFF) else Color(0x16000000),
            thumb = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x3DFFFFFF) else Color(0xF5FFFFFF),
            shadow = if (darkBackdrop) Color(0x80000000) else Color(0x22000000),
            translucent = glassLists && !reduceTransparency,
            reduced = reduceTransparency,
            wallpaper = wallpaper,
            material = material,
        )
    }

    fun wallpaperTop(wallpaper: GlassWallpaper, palette: LiquidPalette, dark: Boolean): Color = when (wallpaper) {
        GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL -> lerp(Color(0xFF0A140E), palette.gradientEnd, 0.35f)
        GlassWallpaper.MIST -> if (dark) lerp(Color(0xFF17191E), palette.gradientEnd, 0.15f) else Color(0xFFE4E8EF)
        GlassWallpaper.LAGOON -> if (dark) Color(0xFF031327) else Color(0xFFE2EFFF)
        GlassWallpaper.DUSK -> if (dark) Color(0xFF1A0712) else Color(0xFFFFF0E6)
        GlassWallpaper.GRAPHITE -> if (dark) Color(0xFF121317) else Color(0xFFE1E5EC)
        GlassWallpaper.PLAIN -> if (dark) lerp(Color.Black, palette.gradientEnd, 0.08f) else Color(0xFFF2F2F7)
    }
}

@Immutable
class GlassLook(
    val colors: GlassColors,
    val material: GlassMaterial = GlassMaterial.CLEAR,
    val wallpaper: GlassWallpaper = GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL,
    val glassLists: Boolean = true,
    val shrinkTabBar: Boolean = true,
    val motionHighlights: Boolean = false,
    val reduceTransparency: Boolean = false,
)

val LocalGlassLook = staticCompositionLocalOf {
    GlassLook(
        colors = GlassPalette.of(
            dark = true,
            palette = LiquidPalettes.SPORT_RED,
            material = GlassMaterial.CLEAR,
            wallpaper = GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL,
            glassLists = true,
            reduceTransparency = false,
        )
    )
}

object Glass {
    val colors: GlassColors
        @Composable @ReadOnlyComposable get() = LocalGlassLook.current.colors

    val look: GlassLook
        @Composable @ReadOnlyComposable get() = LocalGlassLook.current
}

@Stable
class GlassChromeBackdrops(
    val wall: LayerBackdrop,
    val page: LayerBackdrop,
    val combined: Backdrop,
)

val LocalGlassChrome = staticCompositionLocalOf<GlassChromeBackdrops?> { null }
val LocalGlassTilt = staticCompositionLocalOf<State<Float>?> { null }

@Composable
fun rememberGlassChrome(): GlassChromeBackdrops {
    val wall = rememberLayerBackdrop()
    val page = rememberLayerBackdrop()
    val combined = rememberCombinedBackdrop(wall, page)
    return remember(wall, page, combined) { GlassChromeBackdrops(wall, page, combined) }
}

private object BotanicalWallpaperCache {
    @Volatile
    var cachedBitmap: ImageBitmap? = null
}

@Composable
fun GlassWallpaperLayer(
    backdrop: LayerBackdrop,
    palette: LiquidPalette,
    isDark: Boolean,
    wallpaper: GlassWallpaper,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val paletteStart by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = palette.gradientStart,
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 450),
        label = "bg_palette_start"
    )
    val paletteEnd by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = palette.gradientEnd,
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 450),
        label = "bg_palette_end"
    )
    val paletteAccent by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = palette.getAccent(isDark),
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 450),
        label = "bg_palette_accent"
    )
    val baseTint by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = liquidBackgroundNeutralColor(palette, isDark),
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 350),
        label = "bg_base_tint"
    )

    val botanicalBitmap by produceState<ImageBitmap?>(
        initialValue = BotanicalWallpaperCache.cachedBitmap,
        key1 = wallpaper == GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL
    ) {
        if (wallpaper == GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL && value == null) {
            value = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                BotanicalWallpaperCache.cachedBitmap ?: run {
                    val opts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply {
                        inPreferredConfig = Bitmap.Config.RGB_565
                    }
                    BitmapFactory.decodeResource(context.resources, R.drawable.img_liquid_glass_bg, opts)
                        ?.asImageBitmap()
                        ?.also { BotanicalWallpaperCache.cachedBitmap = it }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    val atmosphericBrush = remember(isDark, baseTint, paletteStart, paletteEnd, paletteAccent) {
        if (isDark) {
            Brush.verticalGradient(
                colors = listOf(
                    paletteEnd.copy(alpha = 0.62f),
                    baseTint.copy(alpha = 0.44f),
                    paletteAccent.copy(alpha = 0.16f)
                )
            )
        } else {
            val topTint = lerp(paletteStart, Color(0xFF0D1722), 0.30f).copy(alpha = 0.26f)
            val midTint = lerp(paletteAccent, Color(0xFF0D1722), 0.25f).copy(alpha = 0.12f)
            val botTint = lerp(paletteEnd, Color(0xFF0D1722), 0.30f).copy(alpha = 0.28f)
            Brush.verticalGradient(colors = listOf(topTint, midTint, botTint))
        }
    }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .layerBackdrop(backdrop)
            .drawBehind {
                if (wallpaper != GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL) {
                    drawGlassWallpaper(wallpaper, isDark, paletteAccent)
                }
            }
    ) {
        if (wallpaper == GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL) {
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(Color(0xFF0B1A10))
            )
            botanicalBitmap?.let { bmp ->
                Image(
                    bitmap = bmp,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    contentScale = ContentScale.Crop,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                )
            }
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(atmosphericBrush)
            )
        }
    }
}

fun DrawScope.drawGlassWallpaper(
    kind: GlassWallpaper,
    dark: Boolean,
    paletteTint: Color = Color(0xFFFF3355),
) {
    when (kind) {
        GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL -> {
            drawRect(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    0f to Color(0xFF0D2818),
                    0.5f to Color(0xFF153D24),
                    1f to Color(0xFF091A10)
                )
            )
            ellipseGlow(0.75f, 0.2f, 0.7f, 0.38f, Color(0x552EA058))
            ellipseGlow(0.22f, 0.75f, 0.7f, 0.42f, paletteTint.copy(alpha = 0.20f))
        }
        GlassWallpaper.MIST -> {
            drawRect(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    0f to (if (dark) lerp(Color(0xFF17191E), paletteTint, 0.12f) else lerp(Color(0xFFE4E8EF), paletteTint, 0.05f)),
                    0.52f to (if (dark) Color(0xFF0B0C0F) else Color(0xFFECEEF3)),
                    1f to (if (dark) Color.Black else Color(0xFFF2F2F7)),
                )
            )
            ellipseGlow(0.82f, 0.12f, 0.75f, 0.35f, paletteTint.copy(alpha = if (dark) 0.22f else 0.10f))
        }
        GlassWallpaper.LAGOON -> {
            drawRect(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    listOf(
                        if (dark) Color(0xFF031327) else Color(0xFFE2EFFF),
                        if (dark) Color(0xFF010812) else Color(0xFFEEF5FA),
                    )
                )
            )
            ellipseGlow(0.82f, 0.06f, 0.85f, 0.42f, if (dark) Color(0x730091FF) else Color(0x5C0088FF))
            ellipseGlow(0.08f, 0.58f, 0.80f, 0.40f, if (dark) Color(0x4700D2E0) else Color(0x4700C3D0))
        }
        GlassWallpaper.DUSK -> {
            drawRect(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    listOf(
                        if (dark) Color(0xFF1A0712) else Color(0xFFFFF0E6),
                        if (dark) Color(0xFF0A0410) else Color(0xFFF6EDFB),
                    )
                )
            )
            ellipseGlow(0.20f, 0.06f, 0.85f, 0.42f, if (dark) Color(0x61FF9230) else Color(0x5CFF8D28))
            ellipseGlow(0.90f, 0.60f, 0.80f, 0.40f, if (dark) Color(0x52DB34F2) else Color(0x3DCB30E0))
        }
        GlassWallpaper.GRAPHITE -> {
            drawRect(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    listOf(
                        if (dark) Color(0xFF121317) else Color(0xFFE1E5EC),
                        if (dark) Color(0xFF050506) else Color(0xFFECEEF2),
                    )
                )
            )
            ellipseGlow(0.80f, 0.08f, 0.85f, 0.42f, if (dark) Color(0x3D98989D) else Color(0xE6FFFFFF))
        }
        GlassWallpaper.PLAIN -> drawRect(if (dark) Color.Black else Color(0xFFF2F2F7))
    }
}

private fun DrawScope.ellipseGlow(cx: Float, cy: Float, rx: Float, ry: Float, color: Color) {
    val radiusX = size.width * rx
    val radiusY = size.height * ry
    if (radiusX <= 0f || radiusY <= 0f) return
    val center = Offset(size.width * cx, size.height * cy)
    scale(scaleX = radiusX / radiusY, scaleY = 1f, pivot = center) {
        drawCircle(
            Brush.radialGradient(
                0f to color,
                0.72f to color.copy(alpha = 0f),
                center = center,
                radius = radiusY,
            ),
            radius = radiusY,
            center = center,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun GlassTopEdge(
    backdrop: Backdrop?,
    palette: LiquidPalette,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val top = WindowInsets.statusBars.asPaddingValues().calculateTopPadding()
    if (top <= 0.dp) return
    val height = top + 14.dp
    val tone = GlassPalette.wallpaperTop(c.wallpaper, palette, c.dark)

    if (backdrop == null || c.reduced || !isGlassRenderEffectSupported()) {
        Box(
            modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(height)
                .drawBehind {
                    drawRect(
                        Brush.verticalGradient(
                            0f to tone.copy(alpha = 0.88f),
                            0.55f to tone.copy(alpha = 0.64f),
                            1f to tone.copy(alpha = 0f),
                        ),
                    )
                },
        )
        return
    }

    val fade = Brush.verticalGradient(0f to Color.Black, 0.55f to Color.Black, 1f to Color.Transparent)
    Box(
        modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(height)
            .drawBackdrop(
                backdrop = backdrop,
                shape = { RectangleShape },
                effects = { blur(10.dp.toPx()) },
                highlight = null,
                shadow = null,
                onDrawSurface = { drawRect(tone.copy(alpha = 0.34f)) },
                onDrawFront = { drawRect(fade, blendMode = BlendMode.DstIn) },
            ),
    )
}

fun glassRimBrush(c: GlassColors): Brush = Brush.linearGradient(
    0f to c.rimHi,
    0.28f to c.rimMid,
    0.48f to Color.Transparent,
    0.64f to Color.Transparent,
    1f to c.rimLo,
)

fun Modifier.glassRim(shape: Shape, c: GlassColors, width: Dp = 1.dp): Modifier =
    border(width, glassRimBrush(c), shape)

fun Modifier.glassHighlight(shape: Shape, color: Color, thickness: Dp = 1.dp): Modifier = drawWithCache {
    val outline = shape.createOutline(size, layoutDirection, this)
    val inner = Path().apply { addOutline(outline) }
    val shifted = Path().apply {
        addOutline(outline)
        translate(Offset(0f, thickness.toPx()))
    }
    onDrawWithContent {
        clipPath(inner) {
            clipPath(shifted, ClipOp.Difference) { drawRect(color) }
        }
        drawContent()
    }
}

fun glassHighlightOf(tilt: State<Float>?): Highlight =
    if (tilt == null) {
        Highlight.Default
    } else {
        Highlight(style = HighlightStyle.Default(angle = tilt.value))
    }

fun Modifier.glassChrome(
    c: GlassColors,
    shape: Shape,
    backdrop: Backdrop? = null,
    surface: Color = c.chrome,
    blurRadius: Dp = 12.dp,
    refraction: Boolean = true,
    elevated: Boolean = true,
    tilt: State<Float>? = null,
    useTranslucent: Boolean = true,
): Modifier {
    val renderBlur = backdrop != null && !c.reduced && useTranslucent && isGlassRenderEffectSupported()
    if (!renderBlur) {
        val fill = when {
            c.reduced || !useTranslucent -> c.chromeSolid
            else -> lerp(c.chromeSolid, surface, 0.45f).copy(alpha = maxOf(surface.alpha, 0.78f))
        }
        return this
            .then(
                if (elevated) {
                    Modifier.shadow(8.dp, shape, clip = false, ambientColor = c.shadow, spotColor = c.shadow)
                } else {
                    Modifier
                }
            )
            .clip(shape)
            .background(fill)
            .then(
                if (!c.reduced) {
                    Modifier
                        .glassHighlight(shape, c.highlight)
                        .glassRim(shape, c)
                } else {
                    Modifier.border(0.6.dp, c.separator, shape)
                }
            )
    }

    return this.drawBackdrop(
        backdrop = backdrop!!,
        shape = { shape },
        effects = {
            vibrancy()
            blur(blurRadius.toPx())
            if (refraction && isGlassLensSupported()) {
                lens(12.dp.toPx(), 22.dp.toPx())
            }
        },
        highlight = { glassHighlightOf(tilt) },
        shadow = if (elevated) ({ Shadow(radius = 20.dp, color = c.shadow) }) else null,
        onDrawSurface = { drawRect(surface) },
    )
}

private class GlassPressIndication(private val color: Color) : IndicationNodeFactory {
    override fun create(interactionSource: InteractionSource): DelegatableNode =
        GlassPressNode(interactionSource, color)

    override fun equals(other: Any?): Boolean = other is GlassPressIndication && other.color == color
    override fun hashCode(): Int = color.hashCode()
}

private class GlassPressNode(
    private val source: InteractionSource,
    private val color: Color,
) : Modifier.Node(), DrawModifierNode {
    private var pressed = false

    override fun onAttach() {
        coroutineScope.launch {
            var count = 0
            source.interactions.collect { interaction ->
                when (interaction) {
                    is PressInteraction.Press -> count++
                    is PressInteraction.Release, is PressInteraction.Cancel -> count = (count - 1).coerceAtLeast(0)
                }
                val now = count > 0
                if (now != pressed) {
                    pressed = now
                    invalidateDraw()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    override fun ContentDrawScope.draw() {
        if (pressed) drawRect(color)
        drawContent()
    }
}

@Composable
fun glassRowIndication(): Indication {
    val c = Glass.colors
    return remember(c.fill2) { GlassPressIndication(c.fill2) }
}

@Composable
fun Modifier.glassPress(source: InteractionSource, scale: Float = 1.06f): Modifier {
    val pressed by source.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val s by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (pressed) scale else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.5f, stiffness = Spring.StiffnessMedium),
        label = "glass-press",
    )
    return graphicsLayer {
        scaleX = s
        scaleY = s
    }
}

===== FILE: java/com/example/ui/glass/GlassTilt.kt =====
package com.example.ui.glass

import android.content.Context
import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.hardware.SensorEvent
import android.hardware.SensorEventListener
import android.hardware.SensorManager
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.State
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableFloatStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.lifecycle.compose.LifecycleResumeEffect
import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.sqrt

const val GlassLightAngle = 45f

@Composable
fun rememberGlassTilt(enabled: Boolean): State<Float> {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val angle = remember { mutableFloatStateOf(GlassLightAngle) }
    val manager = remember(context) {
        context.getSystemService(Context.SENSOR_SERVICE) as? SensorManager
    }
    val sensor = remember(manager) {
        manager?.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_GRAVITY)
            ?: manager?.getDefaultSensor(Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER)
    }

    LifecycleResumeEffect(enabled, sensor) {
        if (!enabled || manager == null || sensor == null) {
            angle.floatValue = GlassLightAngle
            return@LifecycleResumeEffect onPauseOrDispose { }
        }

        val listener = object : SensorEventListener {
            override fun onSensorChanged(event: SensorEvent) {
                val x = event.values[0]
                val y = event.values[1]
                val z = event.values[2]
                val roll = Math.toDegrees(
                    atan2(x.toDouble(), sqrt((y * y + z * z).toDouble()))
                ).toFloat()
                val target = (GlassLightAngle + roll * 1.5f).coerceIn(-45f, 135f)
                angle.floatValue = angle.floatValue * 0.8f + target * 0.2f
            }

            override fun onAccuracyChanged(sensor: Sensor?, accuracy: Int) = Unit
        }

        manager.registerListener(listener, sensor, SensorManager.SENSOR_DELAY_UI)
        onPauseOrDispose {
            manager.unregisterListener(listener)
        }
    }

    return angle
}

val LocalLiquidBottomClearance = staticCompositionLocalOf { 0.dp }

/**
 * Root container providing the selected organic or studio Liquid Glass wallpaper
 * and Kyant Backdrop layer hosts.
 */
@Composable
fun LiquidGlassRoot(
    chrome: GlassChromeBackdrops = rememberGlassChrome(),
    motionHighlights: Boolean = Glass.look.motionHighlights,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val look = Glass.look
    val tilt = rememberGlassTilt(enabled = motionHighlights && !c.reduced)

    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        GlassWallpaperLayer(
            backdrop = chrome.wall,
            palette = LiquidTheme.palette,
            isDark = LiquidTheme.isDark,
            wallpaper = look.wallpaper
        )

        Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            CompositionLocalProvider(
                LocalGlassChrome provides chrome,
                LocalGlassTilt provides tilt
            ) {
                content()
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Primary Liquid Glass modifier powered by Kyant Backdrop + ZedSecure crystal highlights:
 * - Real-time Backdrop blur + vibrancy (+ optional lens refraction on API 33+)
 * - Adaptive Clear / Tinted material translucency & Reduce Transparency fallback
 * - Crisp top inner highlight & diagonal specular crystal rim
 */
@Composable
fun Modifier.liquidGlass(
    useBackdrop: Boolean = true,
    backdrop: Backdrop? = LocalGlassChrome.current?.wall,
    shape: Shape = RoundedCornerShape(22.dp),
    tintAlpha: Float = if (LiquidTheme.isDark) 0.38f else 0.40f,
    elevation: Dp = 8.dp,
    borderAlpha: Float = if (LiquidTheme.isDark) 0.68f else 0.78f,
    refraction: Boolean = false
): Modifier {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val look = Glass.look
    val tilt = LocalGlassTilt.current
    val isDark = LiquidTheme.isDark
    val accent = LiquidTheme.accentColor

    val targetAlpha = when {
        c.reduced -> 0.96f
        c.material == GlassMaterial.TINTED -> maxOf(tintAlpha, c.module.alpha)
        c.wallpaper != GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL -> c.module.alpha
        else -> tintAlpha
    }
    val surfaceColor = remember(c.cell, targetAlpha) {
        c.cell.copy(alpha = targetAlpha)
    }

    val renderLiveBlur = useBackdrop && backdrop != null && !c.reduced && look.glassLists && isGlassRenderEffectSupported()

    if (renderLiveBlur && backdrop != null) {
        return this.drawBackdrop(
            backdrop = backdrop,
            shape = { shape },
            effects = {
                vibrancy()
                blur(14.dp.toPx())
                if (refraction && isGlassLensSupported()) {
                    lens(12.dp.toPx(), 22.dp.toPx())
                }
            },
            highlight = { glassHighlightOf(tilt) },
            shadow = if (elevation > 0.dp) {
                {
                    Shadow(
                        radius = elevation * 2f,
                        color = if (c.darkBackdrop) Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.42f) else c.shadow
                    )
                }
            } else {
                null
            },
            onDrawSurface = {
                drawRect(surfaceColor)
                drawRect(
                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                        0f to Color.White.copy(alpha = if (c.darkBackdrop) 0.12f else 0.24f),
                        0.45f to accent.copy(alpha = if (c.darkBackdrop) 0.04f else 0.03f),
                        1f to Color.Transparent
                    )
                )
            }
        )
    }

    val fallbackColor = when {
        c.reduced || !look.glassLists -> c.solidCell
        c.wallpaper != GlassWallpaper.BOTANICAL -> lerp(c.solidCell, surfaceColor, 0.45f).copy(alpha = maxOf(surfaceColor.alpha, 0.86f))
        else -> surfaceColor.copy(alpha = (targetAlpha + if (isDark) 0.30f else 0.28f).coerceAtMost(0.94f))
    }

    val borderBrush = remember(c.rimHi, c.rimMid, c.rimLo, accent, borderAlpha, c.darkBackdrop) {
        Brush.linearGradient(
            colors = listOf(
                c.rimHi.copy(alpha = borderAlpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)),
                accent.copy(alpha = if (c.darkBackdrop) 0.34f else 0.22f),
                c.rimMid,
                c.rimLo
            ),
            start = Offset(0f, 0f),
            end = Offset(Float.POSITIVE_INFINITY, Float.POSITIVE_INFINITY)
        )
    }

    val innerSpecularBrush = remember(c.darkBackdrop, accent) {
        Brush.verticalGradient(
            colors = listOf(
                Color.White.copy(alpha = if (c.darkBackdrop) 0.14f else 0.26f),
                accent.copy(alpha = if (c.darkBackdrop) 0.04f else 0.03f),
                Color.Transparent,
                Color.White.copy(alpha = if (c.darkBackdrop) 0.04f else 0.08f)
            )
        )
    }

    return this
        .then(
            if (elevation > 0.dp) {
                Modifier.shadow(
                    elevation = elevation,
                    shape = shape,
                    spotColor = if (c.darkBackdrop) accent.copy(alpha = 0.24f) else c.shadow,
                    ambientColor = c.shadow
                )
            } else {
                Modifier
            }
        )
        .clip(shape)
        .background(fallbackColor)
        .then(
            if (!c.reduced) {
                Modifier
                    .background(innerSpecularBrush)
                    .glassHighlight(shape, c.highlight)
                    .border(BorderStroke(1.dp, borderBrush), shape = shape)
            } else {
                Modifier.border(0.75.dp, c.separator, shape = shape)
            }
        )
}

/**
 * Liquid tap animation: scales down to 0.95 with a responsive spring and haptic feedback.
 */
@Composable
fun Modifier.liquidTapEffect(
    enabled: Boolean = true,
    onClick: () -> Unit
): Modifier {
    var isPressed by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val haptic = LocalHapticFeedback.current
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isPressed) 0.95f else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.6f, stiffness = 500f),
        label = "liquid_tap"
    )

    return if (!enabled) this else this
        .scale(scale)
        .pointerInput(Unit) {
            detectTapGestures(
                onPress = {
                    isPressed = true
                    tryAwaitRelease()
                    isPressed = false
                },
                onTap = {
                    haptic.performHapticFeedback(HapticFeedbackType.TextHandleMove)
                    onClick()
                }
            )
        }
}

@Composable
fun LiquidSectionHeader(
    title: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    actionText: String? = null,
    onActionClick: (() -> Unit)? = null
) {
    val darkBackdrop = Glass.colors.darkBackdrop
    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Text(
            text = title,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                fontSize = 15.sp,
                shadow = if (darkBackdrop) {
                    androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Shadow(
                        color = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.55f),
                        offset = Offset(0f, 1.5f),
                        blurRadius = 4f
                    )
                } else {
                    null
                }
            ),
            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
        )
        if (actionText != null && onActionClick != null) {
            Text(
                text = actionText,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clickable { onActionClick() }
                    .padding(4.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun GroupedRow(
    title: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    subtitle: String? = null,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    iconTint: Color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
    iconBackground: Color? = null,
    valueText: String? = null,
    showArrow: Boolean = true,
    showDivider: Boolean = true,
    onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null,
    trailingContent: (@Composable () -> Unit)? = null
) {
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val badgeShape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
    val badgeBrush = remember(iconTint, iconBackground) {
        val base = iconBackground ?: iconTint
        Brush.verticalGradient(
            0f to lerp(base, Color.White, 0.24f),
            0.62f to base,
            1f to lerp(base, Color.Black, 0.10f)
        )
    }

    Column(modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .heightIn(min = 52.dp)
                .clickable(
                    interactionSource = interactionSource,
                    indication = glassRowIndication(),
                    enabled = onClick != null
                ) { onClick?.invoke() }
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f, fill = false)
            ) {
                if (icon != null) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(40.dp)
                            .shadow(1.5.dp, badgeShape, clip = false, ambientColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.28f), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                            .clip(badgeShape)
                            .background(badgeBrush)
                            .glassHighlight(badgeShape, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.45f), 0.75.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Icon(icon, null, tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                    }
                }
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                    )
                    if (!subtitle.isNullOrBlank()) {
                        Text(
                            subtitle,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                if (valueText != null) {
                    Text(
                        valueText,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                    )
                }
                if (trailingContent != null) {
                    trailingContent()
                } else if (showArrow && onClick != null) {
                    Icon(
                        Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                        null,
                        tint = LiquidTheme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                        modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
        if (showDivider) {
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(start = if (icon != null) 72.dp else 16.dp)
                    .height(0.5.dp)
                    .background(LiquidTheme.dividerColor)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun GradientHeroCard(
    title: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    subtitle: String? = null,
    gradient: Brush = LiquidTheme.cardGradient,
    badgeText: String? = null,
    content: (@Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit)? = null
) {
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(28.dp)
    Surface(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .shadow(10.dp, shape, spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.35f)),
        shape = shape,
        color = Color.Transparent
    ) {
        Box(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .background(gradient)
                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.28f))
                .border(
                    BorderStroke(
                        1.dp,
                        Brush.linearGradient(
                            listOf(
                                Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                                Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.15f),
                                Color.Transparent
                            )
                        )
                    ),
                    shape
                )
                .padding(20.dp)
        ) {
            Column(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
            ) {
                if (badgeText != null) {
                    Surface(
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.2f)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            badgeText,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = Color.White,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(10.dp, 4.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
                Text(
                    title,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineLarge.copy(fontSize = 24.sp),
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.ExtraBold,
                    color = Color.White
                )
                if (!subtitle.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    Text(
                        subtitle,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.85f)
                    )
                }
                if (content != null) {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    content()
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
        )
    }

    title: String,
    subtitle: String,

                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,

                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)

                    Modifier
                        .size(56.dp)
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                        .background(gradient),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Icon(icon, null, tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp))
                }
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp)) {
                    Text(
                        title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                    Text(
                        subtitle,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                        color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }
            }
            if (badgeText != null) {
                Surface(
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                    color = LiquidTheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        badgeText,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                        color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(8.dp, 4.dp)
                    )
                }
            } else if (trailing != null) {
                trailing()
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Snipzy Liquid Glass Primary Button:
 * - Active scale: 0.95
 * - Highlight border gradient
 * - Accent luster
 */
@Composable
fun LiquidPrimaryButton(
    text: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    enabled: Boolean = true
) {
    val interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val isPressed by interactionSource.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (isPressed) 0.95f else 1f,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.6f, stiffness = 500f),
        label = "btn_scale"
    )
    val accent = LiquidTheme.accentColor
    val paletteStart = LiquidTheme.palette.gradientStart
    val midColor = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.lerp(paletteStart, accent, 0.5f)
    val contentColor = onColorFor(midColor)

    val buttonBrush = Brush.linearGradient(
        colors = listOf(
            paletteStart.copy(alpha = if (enabled) 0.94f else 0.45f),
            accent.copy(alpha = if (enabled) 0.90f else 0.40f)
        )
    )

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(52.dp)
            .glassPress(interactionSource, scale = 1.03f)
            .scale(scale)
            .shadow(if (isPressed) 2.dp else 10.dp, CircleShape, spotColor = accent.copy(alpha = 0.40f))
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(buttonBrush, CircleShape)
            .background(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    0f to Color.White.copy(alpha = if (enabled) 0.26f else 0.10f),
                    0.52f to Color.Transparent
                )
            )
            .glassHighlight(CircleShape, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.55f))
            .clickable(
                interactionSource = interactionSource,
                indication = ripple(),
                enabled = enabled,
                role = Role.Button,
                onClick = onClick
            ),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            if (icon != null) {
                Icon(
                    icon,
                    null,
                    tint = contentColor,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
            }
            Text(
                text,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = contentColor
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LiquidSecondaryButton(
    text: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    icon: ImageVector? = null
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(54.dp)
            .liquidTapEffect { onClick() }
            .liquidGlass(
                shape = CircleShape,
                elevation = 4.dp,
                tintAlpha = if (LiquidTheme.isDark) 0.34f else 0.42f
            ),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
        ) {
            if (icon != null) {
                Icon(icon, null, tint = LiquidTheme.textPrimary, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
            }
            Text(
                text,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LiquidDestructiveButton(
    text: String,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(54.dp)
            .liquidTapEffect { onClick() }
            .liquidGlass(
                shape = CircleShape,
                elevation = 4.dp,
                tintAlpha = 0.25f,
                borderAlpha = 0.35f
            )
            .background(SemanticError.copy(alpha = 0.15f), CircleShape),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Text(
            text = text,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            color = SemanticError
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun LiquidCircleButton(
    icon: ImageVector,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    contentDescription: String? = null,
    badgeCount: Int? = null,
    size: Dp = 48.dp,
    tint: Color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary,
    backdrop: Backdrop? = LocalGlassChrome.current?.wall,
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val tilt = LocalGlassTilt.current
    val source = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .defaultMinSize(minWidth = 48.dp, minHeight = 48.dp)
            .glassPress(source)
            .size(size)
            .glassChrome(
                c = c,
                shape = CircleShape,
                backdrop = backdrop,
                refraction = true,
                tilt = tilt,
                useTranslucent = Glass.look.glassLists
            )
            .semantics {
                if (contentDescription != null) this.contentDescription = contentDescription
            }
            .clickable(source, null, role = Role.Button, onClick = onClick),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Icon(
            icon,
            contentDescription = null,
            tint = tint,
            modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
        )
        if (badgeCount != null && badgeCount > 0) {
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                    .padding(8.dp)
                    .size(10.dp)
                    .background(SemanticError, CircleShape)
                    .border(1.dp, Color.White, CircleShape)
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LiquidSegmentedControl(
    items: List<String>,
    selectedIndex: Int,
    onSelectIndex: (Int) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    if (items.isEmpty()) return
    val c = Glass.colors
    val accent = LiquidTheme.accentColor
    BoxWithConstraints(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(48.dp)
            .liquidGlass(shape = CircleShape, elevation = 4.dp, refraction = false)
            .padding(3.dp)
    ) {
        val slot = maxWidth / items.size
        val activeIdx = selectedIndex.coerceIn(0, items.lastIndex)
        val x by animateDpAsState(
            targetValue = slot * activeIdx,
            animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.72f, stiffness = Spring.StiffnessMediumLow),
            label = "glass_segment_pill"
        )
        val thumbShape = CircleShape
        val thumbFill = if (c.darkBackdrop) {
            accent.copy(alpha = 0.28f)
        } else {
            lerp(c.thumb, accent, 0.14f)
        }
        Box(
            Modifier
                .offset(x = x)
                .width(slot)
                .fillMaxHeight()
                .shadow(3.dp, thumbShape, clip = false, ambientColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.18f), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.18f))
                .clip(thumbShape)
                .background(thumbFill)
                .glassHighlight(thumbShape, c.highlight)
                .border(0.8.dp, accent.copy(alpha = if (c.darkBackdrop) 0.72f else 0.45f), thumbShape)
        )
        Row(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .selectableGroup()
        ) {
            items.forEachIndexed { index, title ->
                val isSelected = index == activeIdx
                Box(
                    Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .fillMaxHeight()
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .selectable(
                            selected = isSelected,
                            interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                            indication = null,
                            role = Role.Tab,
                            onClick = { onSelectIndex(index) }
                        ),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Text(
                        title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                        fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                        color = if (isSelected) (if (c.darkBackdrop) accent else c.label) else LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LiquidStepper(
    value: Double,
    onValueChange: (Double) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    step: Double = 1.0,
    min: Double = 0.0,
    max: Double = 500.0,
    formatString: String = "%.1f"
) {
    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .height(46.dp)
            .liquidGlass(shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), elevation = 3.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        IconButton(
            onClick = { onValueChange((value - step).coerceAtLeast(min)) },
            modifier = Modifier.size(42.dp)
        ) {
            Icon(Icons.Filled.Remove, "کاهش", tint = LiquidTheme.textPrimary)
        }
        Box(
            Modifier
                .padding(horizontal = 8.dp)
                .widthIn(min = 52.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                if (step >= 1.0 && value % 1.0 == 0.0) {
                    PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(value.toInt())
                } else {
                    PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(String.format(java.util.Locale.US, formatString, value))
                },
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
            )
        }
        IconButton(
            onClick = { onValueChange((value + step).coerceAtMost(max)) },
            modifier = Modifier.size(42.dp)
        ) {
            Icon(Icons.Filled.Add, "افزایش", tint = LiquidTheme.textPrimary)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LiquidSearchField(
    value: String,
    onValueChange: (String) -> Unit,
    placeholder: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    trailingAction: (@Composable () -> Unit)? = null
) {
    var isFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(54.dp)
            .liquidGlass(
                shape = CircleShape,
                elevation = if (isFocused) 6.dp else 4.dp,
                borderAlpha = if (isFocused) 0.85f else 0.45f
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
    ) {
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
        ) {
            Icon(
                Icons.Filled.Search,
                null,
                tint = if (isFocused) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                modifier = Modifier.size(22.dp)
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
            Box(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                if (value.isEmpty()) {
                    Text(
                        placeholder,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.6f)
                    )
                }
                BasicTextField(
                    value = value,
                    onValueChange = onValueChange,
                    singleLine = true,
                    textStyle = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                        color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                    ),
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .onFocusChanged { isFocused = it.isFocused }
                )
            }
            if (value.isNotEmpty()) {
                IconButton(
                    onClick = { onValueChange("") },
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        Icons.Filled.Close,
                        null,
                        tint = LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
            if (trailingAction != null) trailingAction()
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LiquidInputField(
    value: String,
    onValueChange: (String) -> Unit,
    label: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    placeholder: String = "",
    keyboardOptions: KeyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions.Default,
    singleLine: Boolean = true,
    trailingText: String? = null,
    leadingIcon: ImageVector? = null
) {
    var isFocused by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val isNumeric = keyboardOptions.keyboardType == KeyboardType.Number ||
            keyboardOptions.keyboardType == KeyboardType.Decimal

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .heightIn(min = 60.dp)
            .liquidGlass(
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                elevation = if (isFocused) 6.dp else 3.dp,
                borderAlpha = if (isFocused) 0.8f else 0.35f
            )
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = if (singleLine) 0.dp else 10.dp),
        contentAlignment = if (singleLine) Alignment.CenterStart else Alignment.TopStart
    ) {
        Row(
            verticalAlignment = if (singleLine) Alignment.CenterVertically else Alignment.Top,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
        ) {
            if (leadingIcon != null) {
                Icon(
                    leadingIcon,
                    null,
                    tint = if (isFocused) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
            }
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    label,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                    color = if (isFocused) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                )
                Box {
                    if (value.isEmpty() && placeholder.isNotEmpty()) {
                        Text(
                            placeholder,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.4f)
                        )
                    }
                    BasicTextField(
                        value = value,
                        onValueChange = { typed ->
                            onValueChange(
                                if (isNumeric) {
                                    PersianNumberHelper.toEnglishDigits(typed)
                                        .replace('٫', '.')
                                        .replace(',', '.')
                                } else typed
                            )
                        },
                        singleLine = singleLine,
                        keyboardOptions = keyboardOptions,
                        textStyle = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge.copy(
                            color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        ),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .onFocusChanged { isFocused = it.isFocused }
                    )
                }
            }
            if (trailingText != null) {
                Text(
                    trailingText,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                    color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

/**
 * ZedSecure iOS Liquid Glass Toggle Switch:
 * - 63dp x 28dp capsule track with 48dp accessible hit area
 * - Squash-and-stretch 37dp x 24dp glowing capsule thumb
 */
@Composable
fun LiquidSwitch(
    checked: Boolean,
    onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    enabled: Boolean = true
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val source = remember { MutableInteractionSource() }
    val pressed by source.collectIsPressedAsState()
    val accent = LiquidTheme.accentColor
    val track by animateColorAsState(
        targetValue = if (checked) accent else c.fill,
        label = "glass_switch_track"
    )
    val thumbX by animateDpAsState(
        targetValue = if (checked) 24.dp else 2.dp,
        animationSpec = spring(dampingRatio = 0.62f, stiffness = Spring.StiffnessMediumLow),
        label = "glass_switch_knob"
    )
    val squash by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (pressed && enabled) 1f else 0f,
        animationSpec = spring(stiffness = Spring.StiffnessMedium),
        label = "glass_switch_press"
    )

    Box(
        modifier = modifier
            .sizeIn(minWidth = 63.dp, minHeight = 48.dp)
            .graphicsLayer { alpha = if (enabled) 1f else 0.45f }
            .toggleable(
                value = checked,
                interactionSource = source,
                indication = null,
                enabled = enabled,
                role = Role.Switch,
                onValueChange = onCheckedChange
            ),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(width = 63.dp, height = 28.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(track)
                .border(0.8.dp, if (checked) accent else c.separator, CircleShape)
        ) {
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .offset(x = thumbX, y = 2.dp)
                    .size(width = 37.dp, height = 24.dp)
                    .graphicsLayer {
                        scaleX = 1f + 0.22f * squash
                        scaleY = 1f + 0.12f * squash
                    }
                    .shadow(3.dp, CircleShape, clip = false, ambientColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.22f), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.22f))
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(lerp(Color.White, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.58f), squash))
                    .then(if (squash > 0.01f) Modifier.glassHighlight(CircleShape, Color.White, 1.dp) else Modifier)
            )
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Snipzy Expandable Dropdown / Card:
 * - Glass header with rotating arrow
 * - Animated expanding content
 */
@Composable
fun LiquidExpandableCard(
    title: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    subtitle: String? = null,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    initiallyExpanded: Boolean = false,
    content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit
) {
    var expanded by remember { mutableStateOf(initiallyExpanded) }
    val arrowRotation by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (expanded) 180f else 0f,
        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = 300),
        label = "dropdown_arrow"
    )

    SolidGroupedCard(modifier = modifier) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clickable { expanded = !expanded }
                .padding(16.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
        ) {
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
            ) {
                if (icon != null) {
                    Box(
                        Modifier
                            .size(38.dp)
                            .liquidGlass(shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp), elevation = 2.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Icon(icon, null, tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                    }
                }
                Column {
                    Text(
                        title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
