                            else -> "تثبیت و تناسب اندام"
                        },
                        showDivider = false,
                        onClick = onNavigateToProfile
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: java/com/example/ui/screens/NutritionScreen.kt =====
package com.example.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyRow
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.itemsIndexed // اضافه شد
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.rememberLazyListState // اضافه شد
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.rememberSaveable
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.example.data.local.*
import com.example.ui.components.*
import com.example.ui.theme.*
import com.example.util.JalaliCalendar
import com.example.util.PersianNumberHelper

@Composable
fun NutritionScreen(
    userSettings: UserSettings,
    selectedDateGregorian: String,
    onSelectDate: (String) -> Unit,
    datesWithNutrition: List<String>,
    currentNutritionLog: NutritionLog?,
    allSupplements: List<Supplement>,
    selectedDateSupplementIntakes: List<SupplementIntakeHistory>,
    allSupplementIntakes: List<SupplementIntakeHistory>,
    onSaveMealsLog: (String, String, String, String, String, String) -> Unit,
    onAddWater: (Int, String) -> Unit,
    onResetWater: (String) -> Unit,
    onSaveSupplement: (Supplement) -> Unit,
    onDeleteSupplement: (Supplement) -> Unit,
    onToggleSupplementActive: (Supplement) -> Unit,
    onToggleSupplementIntake: (Supplement, Boolean, String) -> Unit
) {
    var selectedTab by rememberSaveable { mutableIntStateOf(0) } // 0: وعده‌ها و آب, 1: مکمل‌ها, 2: رژیم هوشمند

    var breakfast by remember(currentNutritionLog) { mutableStateOf(currentNutritionLog?.breakfast ?: "") }
    var lunch by remember(currentNutritionLog) { mutableStateOf(currentNutritionLog?.lunch ?: "") }
    var dinner by remember(currentNutritionLog) { mutableStateOf(currentNutritionLog?.dinner ?: "") }
    var snacks by remember(currentNutritionLog) { mutableStateOf(currentNutritionLog?.snacks ?: "") }
    var dailyNotes by remember(currentNutritionLog) { mutableStateOf(currentNutritionLog?.dailyNotes ?: "") }

    var supplementToEdit by remember { mutableStateOf<Supplement?>(null) }
    var showSupplementDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showIntakeHistoryDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var supplementToDelete by remember { mutableStateOf<Supplement?>(null) }
    var showCalendarPickerDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val todayGregorian = remember { JalaliCalendar.getTodayGregorian() }

    // Smart diet calculation (Mifflin-St Jeor expects kg & cm)
    val rawWeight = (userSettings.weightKg ?: 75.0).coerceAtLeast(1.0)
    val weight = if (userSettings.weightUnit == "lb") rawWeight * 0.45359237 else rawWeight
    val height = (userSettings.heightCm ?: 175.0).coerceAtLeast(50.0)
    val age = userSettings.age ?: 25
    val bmr = (10 * weight) + (6.25 * height) - (5 * age) + if (userSettings.gender == "FEMALE") -161 else 5
    val multiplier = when (userSettings.activityLevel) {
        "SEDENTARY" -> 1.2
        "ACTIVE" -> 1.55
        "ATHLETE" -> 1.725
        else -> 1.375
    }
    val tdee = bmr * multiplier
    val targetCalories = when (userSettings.fitnessGoal) {
        "BULK" -> tdee + 450
        "CUT" -> (tdee - 450).coerceAtLeast(1200.0)
        else -> tdee
    }
    val targetProteinGrams = when (userSettings.fitnessGoal) {
        "BULK" -> weight * 2.1
        "CUT" -> weight * 2.3
        else -> weight * 1.9
    }
    val targetFatGrams = (targetCalories * 0.25) / 9.0
    val targetCarbsGrams = (targetCalories - (targetProteinGrams * 4 + targetFatGrams * 9)).coerceAtLeast(0.0) / 4.0

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = com.example.ui.components.LocalLiquidBottomClearance.current + 20.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        // 1. Inline Liquid Top Bar
        item {
            LiquidTopBar(
                title = "تغذیه، آب و مکمل‌ها",
                navigationIcon = {
                    if (selectedTab == 1) {
                        LiquidCircleButton(
                            icon = Icons.Filled.History,
                            contentDescription = "سوابق مکمل‌ها",
                            onClick = { showIntakeHistoryDialog = true }
                        )
                    }
                },
                actions = {
                    if (selectedTab == 1) {
                        LiquidCircleButton(
                            icon = Icons.Filled.Add,
                            contentDescription = "افزودن مکمل",
                            onClick = {
                                supplementToEdit = null
                                showSupplementDialog = true
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            )
        }

        // 2. Jalali Date Chips Selector (Extended 60-day window with Jump Picker)
        item {
            val daysList = remember {
                val cal = java.util.Calendar.getInstance()
                cal.add(java.util.Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -45)
                val list = mutableListOf<String>()
                val sdf = java.text.SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd", java.util.Locale.US)
                for (i in 0..60) {
                    list.add(sdf.format(cal.time))
                    cal.add(java.util.Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, 1)
                }
                list
            }
            val initialIndex = remember(selectedDateGregorian) {
                val idx = daysList.indexOf(selectedDateGregorian)
                if (idx >= 0) (idx - 2).coerceAtLeast(0) else 43
            }
            val rowState = rememberLazyListState(initialFirstVisibleItemIndex = initialIndex)

            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = JalaliCalendar.formatGregorianToPersian(selectedDateGregorian),
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                    )
                    Surface(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .height(44.dp)
                            .liquidTapEffect { showCalendarPickerDialog = true },
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                        color = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 10.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(Icons.Filled.CalendarMonth, contentDescription = null, tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                            Text("تقویم ماهانه", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LiquidTheme.accentColor)
                        }
                    }
                }

                LazyRow(
                    state = rowState,
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                ) {
                items(daysList) { gDate ->
                    val isSelected = gDate == selectedDateGregorian
                    val isToday = gDate == todayGregorian
                    val hasLog = datesWithNutrition.contains(gDate)
                    val jDate = JalaliCalendar.gregorianToJalali(gDate)

                    Surface(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .height(58.dp)
                            .width(52.dp)
                            .liquidTapEffect { onSelectDate(gDate) },
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                        color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.surfaceSolid
                    ) {
                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = if (isToday) "امروز" else JalaliCalendar.formatWeekdayShort(gDate),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.onAccent.copy(alpha = 0.85f) else LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(jDate.day),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.onAccent else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                            )
                            if (hasLog && !isSelected) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(4.dp)
                                        .clip(CircleShape)
                                        .background(LiquidTheme.accentColor)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        }

        // 3. Segmented Control
        item {
            Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                LiquidSegmentedControl(
                    items = listOf("وعده‌ها و آب", "مکمل‌ها", "رژیم و ماکروها"),
                    selectedIndex = selectedTab,
                    onSelectIndex = { selectedTab = it }
                )
            }
        }

        when (selectedTab) {
            0 -> {
                // Tab 0: Meals & Water
                item {
                    val currentWater = currentNutritionLog?.waterIntakeMl ?: 0
                    val goalWater = userSettings.waterGoalMl.coerceAtLeast(1000)
                    val percent = ((currentWater.toDouble() / goalWater.toDouble()) * 100).toInt().coerceIn(0, 100)

                    Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                        GradientHeroCard(
                            title = "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(currentWater)} از ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(goalWater)} ml آب",
                            subtitle = "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(percent)}٪ از سهمیه روزانه مصرف شده",
                            gradient = LiquidPalettes.OCEAN.getCardGradient(),
                            badgeText = "هیدراتاسیون امروز"
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                            ) {
                                Surface(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .height(42.dp)
                                        .liquidTapEffect { onAddWater(250, selectedDateGregorian) },
                                    shape = CircleShape,
                                    color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.25f)
                                ) {
                                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                        Text("+۲۵۰ ml", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                                    }
                                }

                                Surface(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .height(42.dp)
                                        .liquidTapEffect { onAddWater(500, selectedDateGregorian) },
                                    shape = CircleShape,
                                    color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.25f)
                                ) {
                                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                        Text("+۵۰۰ ml", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                                    }
                                }

                                IconButton(
                                    onClick = { onResetWater(selectedDateGregorian) },
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(42.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Refresh, contentDescription = "ریست", tint = Color.White)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Meals Inset Grouped Form
                item {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "ثبت وعده‌های غذایی")

                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                Column(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .padding(16.dp),
                                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                                ) {
                                    LiquidInputField(
                                        value = breakfast,
                                        onValueChange = { breakfast = it },
                                        label = "صبحانه",
                                        placeholder = "مثلاً ۴ سفیده تخم‌مرغ + جو دوسر و کره بادام"
                                    )

                                    LiquidInputField(
                                        value = lunch,
                                        onValueChange = { lunch = it },
                                        label = "ناهار",
                                        placeholder = "مثلاً ۲۰۰ گرم فیله مرغ + کته و سالاد"
                                    )

                                    LiquidInputField(
                                        value = dinner,
                                        onValueChange = { dinner = it },
                                        label = "شام",
                                        placeholder = "مثلاً ماهی یا گوشت گریل + بروکلی و سیب‌زمینی"
                                    )

                                    LiquidInputField(
                                        value = snacks,
                                        onValueChange = { snacks = it },
                                        label = "میان‌وعده‌ها",
                                        placeholder = "مثلاً شیک پروتئین وی، موز و گردو"
                                    )

                                    LiquidInputField(
                                        value = dailyNotes,
                                        onValueChange = { dailyNotes = it },
                                        label = "یادداشت و وضعیت انرژی",
                                        placeholder = "مثلاً انرژی بالا در تمرین عصر"
                                    )

                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))

                                    LiquidPrimaryButton(
                                        text = "ذخیره وعده‌های این روز",
                                        onClick = {
                                            onSaveMealsLog(
                                                selectedDateGregorian,
                                                breakfast,
                                                lunch,
                                                dinner,
                                                snacks,
                                                dailyNotes
                                            )
                                        }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            1 -> {
                // Tab 1: Supplements
                if (allSupplements.isEmpty()) {
                    item {
                        EmptyStateCard(
                            icon = Icons.Filled.Medication,
                            title = "هیچ مکملی ثبت نشده است",
                            description = "مکمل‌های مصرفی خود مانند پروتئین وی، کراتین یا ویتامین‌ها را اضافه کنید تا یادآوری روزانه فعال شود.",
                            actionButtonText = "افزودن اولین مکمل",
                            onActionClick = {
                                supplementToEdit = null
                                showSupplementDialog = true
                            }
                        )
                    }
                } else {
                    item {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                LiquidSectionHeader(title = "لیست مکمل‌ها و ثبت مصرف")

                                SolidGroupedCard {
                                    allSupplements.forEachIndexed { index, sup ->
                                        val isTaken = selectedDateSupplementIntakes.any { it.supplementId == sup.id }

                                        GroupedRow(
                                            title = sup.name,
                                            subtitle = "دوز: ${sup.dosage} • ساعت یادآور: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(sup.reminderTime)}",
                                            icon = Icons.Filled.Medication,
                                            iconTint = if (sup.isActive) LiquidTheme.iconGreen else LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                                            showArrow = false,
                                            showDivider = index < allSupplements.size - 1,
                                            onClick = {
                                                supplementToEdit = sup
                                                showSupplementDialog = true
                                            },
                                            trailingContent = {
                                                Row(
                                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                                                ) {
                                                    // Checkmark for taken today (Tapping toggles intake)
                                                    Surface(
                                                        modifier = Modifier
                                                            .size(42.dp)
                                                            .liquidTapEffect {
                                                                onToggleSupplementIntake(sup, isTaken, selectedDateGregorian)
                                                            },
                                                        shape = CircleShape,
                                                        color = if (isTaken) SemanticSuccess else LiquidTheme.surfaceSolid,
                                                        border = if (isTaken) null else BorderStroke(1.5.dp, LiquidTheme.dividerColor)
                                                    ) {
                                                        Box(
                                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                                        ) {
                                                            Icon(
                                                                imageVector = Icons.Filled.Check,
                                                                contentDescription = if (isTaken) "مصرف شده (لمس برای لغو)" else "ثبت مصرف امروز",
                                                                tint = if (isTaken) Color.White else LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                                                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                                            )
                                                        }
                                                    }

                                                    // Active Toggle Switch
                                                    LiquidSwitch(
                                                        checked = sup.isActive,
                                                        onCheckedChange = { onToggleSupplementActive(sup) }
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            }
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            2 -> {
                // Tab 2: Smart Diet & Macros
                item {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                        GradientHeroCard(
                            title = "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(targetCalories.toInt())} کیلوکالری در روز",
                            subtitle = when (userSettings.fitnessGoal) {
                                "BULK" -> "هدف حجم عضلانی (۴۵۰+ کالری مازاد)"
                                "CUT" -> "هدف کاهش چربی (۴۵۰- کالری کسری)"
                                else -> "هدف تثبیت وزن و نگهداری"
                            },
                            badgeText = "محاسبه هوشمند ماکروها",
                            gradient = LiquidPalettes.EMERALD.getCardGradient()
                        )
                    }
                }

                item {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "سهمیه تفکیکی ماکرونوترینت‌ها")

                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                GroupedRow(
                                    title = "پروتئین روزانه",
                                    icon = Icons.Filled.FitnessCenter,
                                    iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconRed,
                                    valueText = "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(targetProteinGrams.toInt())} گرم",
                                    subtitle = "۲ تا ۲.۳ گرم به ازای هر کیلوگرم وزن بدن",
                                    showDivider = true,
                                    showArrow = false
                                )

                                GroupedRow(
                                    title = "کربوهیدرات پیچیده",
                                    icon = Icons.Filled.Grain,
                                    iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconOrange,
                                    valueText = "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(targetCarbsGrams.toInt())} گرم",
                                    subtitle = "سوخت اصلی تمرینات قدرتی",
                                    showDivider = true,
                                    showArrow = false
                                )

                                GroupedRow(
                                    title = "چربی‌های مفید",
                                    icon = Icons.Filled.Opacity,
                                    iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconCyan,
                                    valueText = "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(targetFatGrams.toInt())} گرم",
                                    subtitle = "تنظیم هورمون‌ها و سلامت مفاصل",
                                    showDivider = false,
                                    showArrow = false
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Add / Edit Supplement Dialog
    if (showSupplementDialog) {
        var sName by remember { mutableStateOf(supplementToEdit?.name ?: "") }
        var sDosage by remember { mutableStateOf(supplementToEdit?.dosage ?: "") }
        var sTime by remember { mutableStateOf(supplementToEdit?.reminderTime ?: "09:00") }
        var sNotes by remember { mutableStateOf(supplementToEdit?.notes ?: "") }
        var sActive by remember { mutableStateOf(supplementToEdit?.isActive ?: true) }

        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showSupplementDialog = false },
            title = if (supplementToEdit == null) "افزودن مکمل جدید" else "ویرایش مکمل",
            message = "",
            confirmText = "ذخیره مکمل",
            dismissText = "انصراف",
            onConfirm = {
                if (sName.isNotBlank()) {
                    val sup = (supplementToEdit ?: Supplement(name = sName, dosage = sDosage)).copy(
                        name = sName,
                        dosage = sDosage,
                        reminderTime = sTime,
                        notes = sNotes,
                        isActive = sActive
                    )
                    onSaveSupplement(sup)
                }
            },
            content = {
                var showPickerForSupplement by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                    LiquidInputField(
                        value = sName,
                        onValueChange = { sName = it },
                        label = "نام مکمل (مثلاً کراتین، وی)"
                    )
                    LiquidInputField(
                        value = sDosage,
                        onValueChange = { sDosage = it },
                        label = "دوز مصرفی (مثلاً ۵ گرم، ۱ پیمانه)"
                    )

                    // Touchable Glass Time Selector
                    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                        Text("ساعت یادآور مصرف روزانه:", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary)
                        Surface(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(50.dp)
                                .liquidTapEffect { showPickerForSupplement = true },
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                            color = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxSize()
                                    .padding(horizontal = 14.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Filled.AccessTime, contentDescription = null, tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor, modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(sTime),
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                    )
                                }
                                Text(
                                    text = "تغییر ساعت",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = LiquidTheme.accentColor
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    LiquidInputField(
                        value = sNotes,
                        onValueChange = { sNotes = it },
                        label = "یادداشت و نحوه مصرف"
                    )
                }

                if (showPickerForSupplement) {
                    LiquidTimePickerDialog(
                        initialTime = sTime,
                        title = "تنظیم ساعت یادآور مکمل",
                        onDismissRequest = { showPickerForSupplement = false },
                        onConfirm = { picked ->
                            sTime = picked
                            showPickerForSupplement = false
                        }
                    )
                }
            }
        )
    }

    // Supplement Intake History Dialog
    if (showIntakeHistoryDialog) {
        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showIntakeHistoryDialog = false },
            title = "سوابق مصرف مکمل‌ها",
            message = if (allSupplementIntakes.isEmpty()) "هنوز سابقه‌ای ثبت نشده است." else "",
            confirmText = "بستن",
            onConfirm = {},
            content = {
                if (allSupplementIntakes.isNotEmpty()) {
                    LazyColumn(modifier = Modifier.height(240.dp), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                        items(allSupplementIntakes) { h ->
                            val jDate = JalaliCalendar.formatGregorianToPersian(h.date)
                            GroupedRow(
                                title = h.supplementName,
                                subtitle = "$jDate ساعت ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(h.time)}",
                                icon = Icons.Filled.CheckCircle,
                                iconTint = SemanticSuccess,
                                showArrow = false,
                                showDivider = true
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        )
    }

    // Jalali Month & Day Jump Dialog
    if (showCalendarPickerDialog) {
        val currentJalali = remember(selectedDateGregorian) { JalaliCalendar.gregorianToJalali(selectedDateGregorian) }
        var pickedYear by remember { mutableIntStateOf(currentJalali.year) }
        var pickedMonth by remember { mutableIntStateOf(currentJalali.month) }
        var pickedDay by remember { mutableIntStateOf(currentJalali.day) }

        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showCalendarPickerDialog = false },
            title = "انتخاب تاریخ شمسی",
            message = "برای مشاهده یا ثبت وعده‌ها و مکمل‌های روزهای دیگر، تاریخ مورد نظر را انتخاب کنید:",
            confirmText = "مشاهده این تاریخ",
            dismissText = "انصراف",
            onConfirm = {
                val gDate = JalaliCalendar.jalaliToGregorian(pickedYear, pickedMonth, pickedDay)
                onSelectDate(gDate)
                showCalendarPickerDialog = false
            },
            content = {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                ) {
                    // Month selector row
                    Text("ماه:", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary)
                    LazyRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                        itemsIndexed(JalaliCalendar.MONTH_NAMES) { index, mName ->
                            val mNum = index + 1
                            val isSel = pickedMonth == mNum
                            Surface(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .height(44.dp)
                                    .liquidTapEffect {
                                        pickedMonth = mNum
                                        val mDays = JalaliCalendar.getDaysInJalaliMonth(pickedYear, mNum)
                                        if (pickedDay > mDays) pickedDay = mDays
                                    },
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                                color = if (isSel) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                            ) {
                                Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 10.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = mName,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                        fontWeight = if (isSel) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                        color = if (isSel) LiquidTheme.onAccent else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Day selector row
                    val maxDays = JalaliCalendar.getDaysInJalaliMonth(pickedYear, pickedMonth)
                    Text("روز (${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(pickedDay)}):", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary)
                    LazyRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                        items((1..maxDays).toList()) { d ->
                            val isSel = pickedDay == d
                            Surface(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(44.dp)
                                    .liquidTapEffect { pickedDay = d },
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                                color = if (isSel) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                            ) {
                                Box(contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(d),
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                        fontWeight = if (isSel) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                        color = if (isSel) LiquidTheme.onAccent else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        )
    }
}

===== FILE: java/com/example/ui/screens/OnboardingScreen.kt =====
package com.example.ui.screens

import android.net.Uri
import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.rememberScrollState
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.text.KeyboardOptions
import androidx.compose.foundation.verticalScroll
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.rememberSaveable // 👈 اضافه شد
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.layout.ContentScale
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.KeyboardType
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import coil.compose.AsyncImage
import com.example.data.local.UserSettings
import com.example.ui.components.*
import com.example.ui.theme.LiquidTheme
import com.example.util.ImageStorageHelper
import com.example.util.PersianNumberHelper

@Composable
fun OnboardingScreen(
    currentSettings: UserSettings,
    onSaveAndContinue: (name: String, photoUri: String, gender: String, age: Int?, height: Double?, weight: Double?, activity: String, goal: String, meals: Int, water: Int, unit: String) -> Unit,
    onSkip: () -> Unit,
    onRestoreBackupClick: () -> Unit
) {
    var name by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.userName) }
    var photoUri by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.profilePhotoUri) }
    var gender by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.gender.ifBlank { "MALE" }) }
    var ageText by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.age?.toString() ?: "") }
    var heightText by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.heightCm?.toString() ?: "") }
    var weightText by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.weightKg?.toString() ?: "") }
    var activityLevel by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.activityLevel) }
    var fitnessGoal by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.fitnessGoal) }
    var waterGoalText by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.waterGoalMl.toString()) }
    var weightUnit by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.weightUnit) }
    var mealsText by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(currentSettings.mealsCount.toString()) }
    var step by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(0) }
    val lastStep = 2
    BackHandler(enabled = step > 0) { step -= 1 }

    val context = LocalContext.current
    val photoPickerLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()
    ) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            photoUri = ImageStorageHelper.saveProfilePhotoPermanently(context, uri)
        }
    }

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.Transparent,
        topBar = {
            LiquidTopBar(
                title = "راه‌اندازی اولیه",
                endAction = {
                    TextButton(
                        onClick = onSkip,
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("onboarding_skip_button")
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            "رد کردن",
                            color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                        )
                    }
                }
            )
        }
    ) { padding ->
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // نشانگر مرحله
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                for (i in 0..lastStep) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .weight(1f)
                            .height(5.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(
                                if (i <= step) LiquidTheme.accentColor
                                else LiquidTheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.18f)
                            )
                    )
                }
            }
            Text(
                text = "مرحله ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(step + 1)} از ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(lastStep + 1)}",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
            )

            if (step == 0) {
            // Hero Photo Section
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
            ) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .size(100.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated)
                        .border(2.dp, LiquidTheme.accentColor, CircleShape)
                        .clickable { photoPickerLauncher.launch("image/*") }
                        .testTag("onboarding_photo_picker"),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    if (photoUri.isNotBlank()) {
                        AsyncImage(
                            model = photoUri,
                            contentDescription = "عکس پروفایل",
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                        )
                    } else {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Filled.AddAPhoto,
                            contentDescription = "افزودن عکس",
                            tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(36.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                Text(
                    "عکس پروفایل (اختیاری)",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                )
            }

            // Section 1: Basic Info
            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "مشخصات فردی")
            SolidGroupedCard {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                ) {
                    LiquidInputField(
                        value = name,
                        onValueChange = { name = it },
                        label = "نام یا لقب ورزشی",
                        leadingIcon = Icons.Filled.Person,
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("onboarding_name_input")
                    )

                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        LiquidInputField(
                            value = ageText,
                            onValueChange = { ageText = it },
                            label = "سن (سال)",
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("onboarding_age_input")
                        )
                        LiquidInputField(
                            value = heightText,
                            onValueChange = { heightText = it },
                            label = "قد (سانتی‌متر)",
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("onboarding_height_input")
                        )
                    }

                    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            "جنسیت",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                        )
                        LiquidSegmentedControl(
                            items = listOf("آقایان", "بانوان"),
                            selectedIndex = if (gender == "FEMALE") 1 else 0,
                            onSelectIndex = { index -> gender = if (index == 0) "MALE" else "FEMALE" }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // Section 2: Weight & Unit
            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "وزن و مقیاس")
            SolidGroupedCard {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        LiquidInputField(
                            value = weightText,
                            onValueChange = { weightText = it },
                            label = "وزن فعلی ($weightUnit)",
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Decimal),
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("onboarding_weight_input")
                        )
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            LiquidSegmentedControl(
                                items = listOf("کیلوگرم", "پوند"),
                                selectedIndex = if (weightUnit == "lb") 1 else 0,
                                onSelectIndex = { index -> weightUnit = if (index == 0) "kg" else "lb" }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            }

            if (step == 1) {
            // Section 3: Goal
            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "هدف اصلی تمرینی")
            SolidGroupedCard {
                val goals = listOf(
                    "BULK" to ("افزایش حجم عضلانی" to "Bulking - عضله‌سازی و افزایش وزن هدفمند"),
                    "CUT" to ("کاهش چربی و تفکیک" to "Cutting - حفظ عضله و کاهش درصد چربی"),
                    "MAINTAIN" to ("حفظ تناسب و آمادگی" to "Maintenance - تثبیت و سلامت عمومی")
                )
                goals.forEachIndexed { index, (key, info) ->
                    val isSelected = fitnessGoal == key
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clickable { fitnessGoal = key }
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                info.first,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                                fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                            )
                            Text(
                                info.second,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                            )
                        }
                        RadioButton(
                            selected = isSelected,
                            onClick = { fitnessGoal = key },
                            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(
                                selectedColor = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                unselectedColor = LiquidTheme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.5f)
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    if (index < goals.size - 1) {
                        HorizontalDivider(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp),
                            color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor,
                            thickness = 0.5.dp
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // Section 4: Activity Level
            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "سطح فعالیت روزانه")
            SolidGroupedCard {
                val activities = listOf(
                    "SEDENTARY" to ("کم‌تحرک" to "پشت‌میزنشین، کمترین فعالیت فیزیکی"),
                    "MODERATE" to ("متوسط" to "۱ تا ۳ روز ورزش هوازی یا تمرین سبک"),
                    "ACTIVE" to ("پرتحرک" to "۴ تا ۵ روز تمرین ورزشی منظم"),
                    "ATHLETE" to ("ورزشکار حرفه‌ای" to "تمرین سنگین روزانه یا دو نوبت")
                )
                activities.forEachIndexed { index, (key, info) ->
                    val isSelected = activityLevel == key
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clickable { activityLevel = key }
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                info.first,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                                fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                            )
                            Text(
                                info.second,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                            )
                        }
                        RadioButton(
                            selected = isSelected,
                            onClick = { activityLevel = key },
                            colors = RadioButtonDefaults.colors(
                                selectedColor = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                unselectedColor = LiquidTheme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.5f)
                            )
                        )
                    }
                    if (index < activities.size - 1) {
                        HorizontalDivider(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 16.dp),
                            color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor,
                            thickness = 0.5.dp
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            }

            if (step == 2) {
            // Section 5: Hydration & Nutrition Targets
            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "اهداف تغذیه و آب")
            SolidGroupedCard {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        LiquidInputField(
                            value = waterGoalText,
                            onValueChange = { waterGoalText = it },
                            label = "هدف آب (میلی‌لیتر)",
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        )
                        LiquidInputField(
                            value = mealsText,
                            onValueChange = { mealsText = it },
                            label = "تعداد وعده‌ها",
                            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            if (step < lastStep) {
                LiquidPrimaryButton(
                    text = "بعدی",
                    onClick = { step += 1 },
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("onboarding_next_button")
                )
                if (step > 0) {
                    LiquidSecondaryButton(
                        text = "قبلی",
                        onClick = { step -= 1 },
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("onboarding_back_button")
                    )
                }
            } else {
            LiquidPrimaryButton(
                text = "شروع و ذخیره اطلاعات",
                onClick = {
                    val age = PersianNumberHelper.toEnglishDigits(ageText).toIntOrNull()
                    val height = PersianNumberHelper.toEnglishDigits(heightText).toDoubleOrNull()
                    val weight = PersianNumberHelper.toEnglishDigits(weightText).toDoubleOrNull()
                    val water = PersianNumberHelper.toEnglishDigits(waterGoalText).toIntOrNull() ?: 2500
                    val meals = (PersianNumberHelper.toEnglishDigits(mealsText).toIntOrNull() ?: 4).coerceIn(1, 10)
                    onSaveAndContinue(
                        name,
                        photoUri,
                        gender,
                        age,
                        height,
                        weight,
                        activityLevel,
                        fitnessGoal,
                        meals,
                        water,
                        weightUnit
                    )
                },
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("onboarding_save_button")
            )

            LiquidSecondaryButton(
                text = "بازیابی از فایل پشتیبان",
                icon = Icons.Filled.Restore,
                onClick = onRestoreBackupClick,
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("onboarding_restore_button")
            )

            LiquidSecondaryButton(
                text = "قبلی",
                onClick = { step -= 1 },
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("onboarding_back_button")
            )
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: java/com/example/ui/screens/PlanEditorScreen.kt =====
package com.example.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.animation.*
import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.compose.foundation.BorderStroke
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyRow
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.itemsIndexed
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.automirrored.filled.ArrowBack
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.rememberSaveable // 👈 اضافه شد
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.example.data.local.WorkoutExercise
import com.example.data.local.WorkoutPlan
import com.example.ui.components.*
import com.example.ui.theme.*
import com.example.util.JalaliCalendar
import com.example.util.PersianNumberHelper

private data class EditableExerciseItem(
    val uiId: Long,
    val exercise: WorkoutExercise
)

@Composable
fun PlanEditorScreen(
    initialPlan: WorkoutPlan?,
    initialExercises: List<WorkoutExercise>,
    onSave: (WorkoutPlan, List<WorkoutExercise>) -> Unit,
    onBack: () -> Unit
) {
    var name by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(initialPlan?.name ?: "") }
    var notes by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(initialPlan?.notes ?: "") }
    val initialDays = remember {
        initialPlan?.scheduledDay?.split(",")?.map { it.trim() }?.filter { it.isNotBlank() } ?: emptyList()
    }
    var selectedDays by remember { mutableStateOf(initialDays) }

    var nextExerciseUiId by remember {
        mutableLongStateOf((initialExercises.size + 2).toLong())
    }
    var exerciseItems by remember {
        mutableStateOf(
            if (initialExercises.isNotEmpty()) {
                initialExercises.mapIndexed { idx, ex -> EditableExerciseItem(uiId = (idx + 1).toLong(), exercise = ex) }
            } else {
                listOf(
                    EditableExerciseItem(
                        uiId = 1L,
                        exercise = WorkoutExercise(planId = 0, name = "", sets = 3, reps = 10, weight = 20.0)
                    )
                )
            }
        )
    }
    val exercises by remember { derivedStateOf { exerciseItems.map { it.exercise } } }

    val weekDays = JalaliCalendar.WEEKDAY_NAMES

    // خروج بدون ذخیره: اگر چیزی تغییر کرده باشد قبل از خروج تأیید گرفته می‌شود
    var showDiscardDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val hasUnsavedChanges = name != (initialPlan?.name ?: "") ||
        notes != (initialPlan?.notes ?: "") ||
        selectedDays != initialDays ||
        (initialExercises.isNotEmpty() && exercises != initialExercises) ||
        (initialExercises.isEmpty() && exercises.any { it.name.isNotBlank() })
    val requestBack: () -> Unit = {
        if (hasUnsavedChanges) showDiscardDialog = true else onBack()
    }
    BackHandler(onBack = requestBack)

    if (showDiscardDialog) {
        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showDiscardDialog = false },
            title = "خروج بدون ذخیره؟",
            message = "تغییرات این برنامه ذخیره نشده و از بین می‌رود.",
            confirmText = "خروج",
            dismissText = "ادامه ویرایش",
            isDestructive = true,
            onConfirm = onBack
        )
    }

    Scaffold(
        containerColor = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.Transparent,
        contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0, 0, 0, 0),
        topBar = {
            LiquidTopBar(
                title = if (initialPlan == null) "برنامه جدید" else "ویرایش برنامه",
                navigationIcon = {
                    LiquidCircleButton(
                        icon = Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                        contentDescription = "بازگشت",
                        onClick = requestBack
                    )
                },
                actions = {
                    Text(
                        text = "ذخیره",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = if (name.isNotBlank()) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clickable(enabled = name.isNotBlank()) {
                                val plan = (initialPlan ?: WorkoutPlan(name = name)).copy(
                                    name = name,
                                    notes = notes,
                                    scheduledDay = selectedDays.joinToString(",")
                                )
                                onSave(plan, exercises.filter { it.name.isNotBlank() })
                            }
                            .padding(8.dp)
                            .testTag("plan_editor_save_btn")
                    )
                }
            )
        }
    ) { padding ->
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(padding),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // Group 1: General Info
            item {
                LiquidSectionHeader(title = "مشخصات کلی")
                SolidGroupedCard {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(16.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                    ) {
                        LiquidInputField(
                            value = name,
                            onValueChange = { name = it },
                            label = "نام برنامه (ضروری)",
                            placeholder = "مثلاً سینه و بازو، PPL، فول‌بادی"
                        )

                        LiquidInputField(
                            value = notes,
                            onValueChange = { notes = it },
                            label = "توضیحات و نکات تمرینی (اختیاری)",
                            placeholder = "مثلاً استراحت ۹۰ ثانیه بین ست‌ها"
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // Group 2: Scheduled Days
            item {
                LiquidSectionHeader(title = "روزهای اجرا در هفته")
                SolidGroupedCard {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(16.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                        ) {
                            weekDays.take(4).forEach { day ->
                                val isSelected = selectedDays.contains(day)
                                Surface(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .height(40.dp)
                                        .liquidTapEffect {
                                            selectedDays = if (isSelected) selectedDays - day else selectedDays + day
                                        },
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                                    color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                ) {
                                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = day,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                            fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                            color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.onAccent else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                        ) {
                            weekDays.drop(4).forEach { day ->
                                val isSelected = selectedDays.contains(day)
                                Surface(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .height(40.dp)
                                        .liquidTapEffect {
                                            selectedDays = if (isSelected) selectedDays - day else selectedDays + day
                                        },
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                                    color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                ) {
                                    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = day,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                            fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
                                            color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.onAccent else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Group 3: Exercises
            item {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    LiquidSectionHeader(title = "حرکات تمرینی (${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(exercises.size)})")
                    Text(
                        text = "+ حرکت جدید",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                        color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clickable {
                                val newId = nextExerciseUiId++
                                exerciseItems = exerciseItems + EditableExerciseItem(
                                    uiId = newId,
                                    exercise = WorkoutExercise(
                                        planId = initialPlan?.id ?: 0,
                                        name = "",
                                        sets = 3,
                                        reps = 10,
                                        weight = 0.0
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                            .padding(end = 16.dp)
                    )
                }
            }

            itemsIndexed(exerciseItems, key = { _, item -> item.uiId }) { index, item ->
                val exercise = item.exercise
                var expanded by remember(item.uiId) { mutableStateOf(true) }

                SolidGroupedCard(
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
                ) {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(14.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        // Header row with exercise name & collapsible chevron
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            ) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(30.dp)
                                        .clip(CircleShape)
                                        .background(LiquidTheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.16f)),
                                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(index + 1),
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        color = LiquidTheme.accentColor
                                    )
                                }

                                Text(
                                    text = if (exercise.name.isNotBlank()) exercise.name else "حرکت شماره ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(index + 1)}",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                )
                            }

                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                // Move Up
                                if (index > 0) {
                                    IconButton(
                                        onClick = {
                                            val list = exerciseItems.toMutableList()
                                            val temp = list[index]
                                            list[index] = list[index - 1]
                                            list[index - 1] = temp
                                            exerciseItems = list
                                        },
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowUpward, contentDescription = "بالا", tint = LiquidTheme.textSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                                    }
                                }

                                // Move Down
                                if (index < exerciseItems.size - 1) {
                                    IconButton(
                                        onClick = {
                                            val list = exerciseItems.toMutableList()
                                            val temp = list[index]
                                            list[index] = list[index + 1]
                                            list[index + 1] = temp
                                            exerciseItems = list
                                        },
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(Icons.Filled.ArrowDownward, contentDescription = "پایین", tint = LiquidTheme.textSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                                    }
                                }

                                // Duplicate / Clone
                                IconButton(
                                    onClick = {
                                        val list = exerciseItems.toMutableList()
                                        val copyEx = exercise.copy(
                                            id = 0,
                                            name = if (exercise.name.isNotBlank()) "${exercise.name} (کپی)" else ""
                                        )
                                        list.add(index + 1, EditableExerciseItem(uiId = nextExerciseUiId++, exercise = copyEx))
                                        exerciseItems = list
                                    },
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Filled.ContentCopy, contentDescription = "تکثیر حرکت", tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor, modifier = Modifier.size(17.dp))
                                }

                                // Delete
                                IconButton(
                                    onClick = {
                                        exerciseItems = exerciseItems.filterIndexed { i, _ -> i != index }
                                    },
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(Icons.Filled.DeleteOutline, contentDescription = "حذف", tint = SemanticError, modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp))
                                }

                                // Expand/Collapse
                                IconButton(
                                    onClick = { expanded = !expanded },
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = if (expanded) Icons.Filled.ExpandLess else Icons.Filled.ExpandMore,
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                        tint = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        AnimatedVisibility(visible = expanded) {
                            Column(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                            ) {
                                LiquidInputField(
                                    value = exercise.name,
                                    onValueChange = { newName ->
                                        exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                            if (i == index) it.copy(exercise = it.exercise.copy(name = newName)) else it
                                        }
                                    },
                                    label = "نام حرکت",
                                    placeholder = "مثلاً پرس سینه با هالتر"
                                )

                                // 1. Sets count with visual chips
                                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                    ) {
                                        Text("تعداد ست‌ها:", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold, color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary)
                                        LiquidStepper(
                                            value = exercise.sets.toDouble(),
                                            onValueChange = { newSets ->
                                                exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                                    if (i == index) it.copy(exercise = it.exercise.copy(sets = newSets.toInt())) else it
                                                }
                                            },
                                            step = 1.0,
                                            min = 1.0,
                                            max = 20.0
                                        )
                                    }

                                    // Visual set badges
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        (1..exercise.sets).take(7).forEach { setNum ->
                                            Box(
                                                modifier = Modifier
                                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                                    .background(LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated)
                                                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 3.dp)
                                            ) {
                                                Text(
