                                                    text = "ست ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(setNum)}",
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                        if (exercise.sets > 7) {
                                            Text("...", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary)
                                        }
                                    }
                                }

                                HorizontalDivider(color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor, thickness = 0.5.dp)

                                // 2. Timed exercise toggle (Plank, Stretches, Isometric)
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = "حرکت زمان‌دار / تایمری (مثل پلانک)",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                        )
                                        Text(
                                            text = "در زمان تمرین، تایمر معکوس با آلارم صوتی فعال می‌شود",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                        )
                                    }
                                    LiquidSwitch(
                                        checked = exercise.isTimedExercise,
                                        onCheckedChange = { isTimed ->
                                            exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                                if (i == index) it.copy(
                                                    exercise = it.exercise.copy(
                                                        isTimedExercise = isTimed,
                                                        reps = if (isTimed && it.exercise.reps < 10) 60 else it.exercise.reps,
                                                        repsMax = if (isTimed) null else it.exercise.repsMax
                                                    )
                                                ) else it
                                            }
                                        }
                                    )
                                }

                                if (exercise.isTimedExercise) {
                                    // Timed exercise duration selector
                                    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Text(
                                                "مدت زمان نگه داشتن (ثانیه):",
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                                color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                            )
                                            LiquidStepper(
                                                value = exercise.reps.toDouble(),
                                                onValueChange = { newSec ->
                                                    exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                                        if (i == index) it.copy(exercise = it.exercise.copy(reps = newSec.toInt())) else it
                                                    }
                                                },
                                                step = 5.0,
                                                min = 5.0,
                                                max = 600.0
                                            )
                                        }

                                        // Preset quick chips
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                                        ) {
                                            listOf(30, 45, 60, 90, 120).forEach { sec ->
                                                val isSelected = exercise.reps == sec
                                                Surface(
                                                    modifier = Modifier
                                                        .weight(1f)
                                                        .height(40.dp)
                                                        .liquidTapEffect {
                                                            exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                                                if (i == index) it.copy(exercise = it.exercise.copy(reps = sec)) else it
                                                            }
                                                        },
                                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                                                    color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                                ) {
                                                    Box(contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                                        Text(
                                                            text = "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(sec)}ث",
                                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                                            fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                                            color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.onAccent else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                                        )
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    // 3. Regular Reps & Rep Range (e.g. 15 to 20 reps)
                                    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Text(
                                                "حداقل تکرار:",
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                                color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                            )
                                            LiquidStepper(
                                                value = exercise.reps.toDouble(),
                                                onValueChange = { newReps ->
                                                    val r = newReps.toInt()
                                                    exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                                        if (i == index) {
                                                            val curMax = it.exercise.repsMax
                                                            it.copy(
                                                                exercise = it.exercise.copy(
                                                                    reps = r,
                                                                    repsMax = if (curMax != null && curMax <= r) null else curMax
                                                                )
                                                            )
                                                        } else it
                                                    }
                                                },
                                                step = 1.0,
                                                min = 1.0,
                                                max = 100.0
                                            )
                                        }

                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Column {
                                                Text(
                                                    "حداکثر تکرار (بازه تکرار):",
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                                )
                                                if (exercise.repsMax != null && exercise.repsMax > exercise.reps) {
                                                    Text(
                                                        "دامنه: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(exercise.reps)} الی ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(exercise.repsMax)} تکرار",
                                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                                        color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            }
                                            LiquidStepper(
                                                value = (exercise.repsMax ?: exercise.reps).toDouble(),
                                                onValueChange = { newMax ->
                                                    exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                                        if (i == index) it.copy(
                                                            exercise = it.exercise.copy(
                                                                repsMax = if (newMax.toInt() > it.exercise.reps) newMax.toInt() else null
                                                            )
                                                        ) else it
                                                    }
                                                },
                                                step = 1.0,
                                                min = exercise.reps.toDouble(),
                                                max = 100.0
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }

                                HorizontalDivider(color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor, thickness = 0.5.dp)

                                // 4. Weight
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Text("وزنه پیشنهادی (kg):", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary)
                                    LiquidStepper(
                                        value = exercise.weight,
                                        onValueChange = { newWeight ->
                                            exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                                if (i == index) it.copy(exercise = it.exercise.copy(weight = newWeight)) else it
                                            }
                                        },
                                        step = 2.5,
                                        min = 0.0,
                                        max = 500.0
                                    )
                                }

                                LiquidInputField(
                                    value = exercise.notes,
                                    onValueChange = { newNotes ->
                                        exerciseItems = exerciseItems.mapIndexed { i, it ->
                                            if (i == index) it.copy(exercise = it.exercise.copy(notes = newNotes)) else it
                                        }
                                    },
                                    label = "نکات حرکت (اختیاری)",
                                    placeholder = "مثلاً تمرکز روی بخش منفی، مکث در اوج"
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Quick Exercise Suggestions
            item {
                var selectedMuscleGroup by remember { mutableStateOf("سینه") }
                val presetsMap = remember {
                    mapOf(
                        "سینه" to listOf("پرس سینه با هالتر", "بالاسینه با دمبل", "قفسه سینه دمبل", "شنا سوئدی", "پارالل سینه"),
                        "زیربغل" to listOf("زیربغل سیم‌کش از جلو", "بارفیکس", "زیربغل هالتر خم", "قایقی سیم‌کش", "ددلیفت"),
                        "سرشانه" to listOf("پرس سرشانه دمبل", "نشر از جانب", "نشر خم سیم‌کش", "فیس پول", "کول هالتر"),
                        "بازو" to listOf("جلو بازو هالتر", "جلو بازو دمبل", "پشت بازو سیم‌کش طناب", "پشت بازو دیپ", "جلو بازو چکشی"),
                        "پا" to listOf("اسکات با هالتر", "پرس پا دستگاه", "جلو پا ماشین", "پشت پا خوابیده", "ساق پا ایستاده"),
                        "شکم" to listOf("پلانک شکم", "کرانچ روی تشک", "زیرشکم خلبانی", "چرخش روسی")
                    )
                }

                SolidGroupedCard(shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp)) {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(14.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "💡 افزودن سریع حرکات پیشنهادی",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "برای افزودن لمس کنید",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                            )
                        }

                        // Category tabs
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                        ) {
                            presetsMap.keys.forEach { muscle ->
                                val isSelected = selectedMuscleGroup == muscle
                                Surface(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .weight(1f)
                                        .height(40.dp)
                                        .liquidTapEffect { selectedMuscleGroup = muscle },
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                                    color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.accentColor else LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                ) {
                                    Box(contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = muscle,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                            fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                                            color = if (isSelected) LiquidTheme.onAccent else LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        // Exercise chips for selected category
                        val currentList = presetsMap[selectedMuscleGroup] ?: emptyList()
                        LazyRow(
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                        ) {
                            items(currentList) { exName ->
                                Surface(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .liquidTapEffect {
                                            val isTimed = exName.contains("پلانک")
                                            exerciseItems = exerciseItems + EditableExerciseItem(
                                                uiId = nextExerciseUiId++,
                                                exercise = WorkoutExercise(
                                                    planId = initialPlan?.id ?: 0,
                                                    name = exName,
                                                    sets = if (isTimed) 3 else 4,
                                                    reps = if (isTimed) 60 else 10,
                                                    weight = if (isTimed) 0.0 else 20.0,
                                                    isTimedExercise = isTimed
                                                )
                                            )
                                        },
                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                                    color = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated,
                                    border = BorderStroke(0.5.dp, LiquidTheme.dividerColor)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            Icons.Filled.Add,
                                            contentDescription = null,
                                            tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
                                        )
                                        Text(
                                            text = exName,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Prominent Full-Width Add Exercise Button
            item {
                Surface(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .liquidTapEffect {
                            exerciseItems = exerciseItems + EditableExerciseItem(
                                uiId = nextExerciseUiId++,
                                exercise = WorkoutExercise(
                                    planId = initialPlan?.id ?: 0,
                                    name = "",
                                    sets = 3,
                                    reps = 10,
                                    weight = 0.0
                                )
                            )
                        },
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                    color = LiquidTheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.12f),
                    border = BorderStroke(1.5.dp, LiquidTheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 16.dp, horizontal = 20.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Filled.AddCircle,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "+ افزودن حرکت جدید به این برنامه (حرکت ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(exercises.size + 1)})",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = LiquidTheme.accentColor
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            item {
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                LiquidPrimaryButton(
                    text = "ذخیره تغییرات برنامه",
                    onClick = {
                        if (name.isNotBlank()) {
                            val plan = (initialPlan ?: WorkoutPlan(name = name)).copy(
                                name = name,
                                notes = notes,
                                scheduledDay = selectedDays.joinToString(",")
                            )
                            onSave(plan, exercises.filter { it.name.isNotBlank() })
                        }
                    },
                    enabled = name.isNotBlank()
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: java/com/example/ui/screens/ProfileSettingsScreen.kt =====
package com.example.ui.screens

import android.Manifest
import android.content.ClipData
import android.content.ClipboardManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.Build
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.animation.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.border
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.interaction.MutableInteractionSource
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.rememberScrollState
import androidx.compose.foundation.selection.selectable
import androidx.compose.foundation.selection.selectableGroup
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape

import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag

import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.Role
import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.contentDescription
import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.role
import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.selected

import androidx.lifecycle.LifecycleEventObserver

@Composable
fun ProfileSettingsScreen(
    userSettings: UserSettings,
    onSelectTheme: (String) -> Unit,
    onSetAppearanceAndPalette: (appearanceMode: String, accentPalette: String) -> Unit,
    onToggleReminder: (type: String, enabled: Boolean, timeStr: String) -> Unit,
    onTestNotification: () -> Unit,
    onExportBackupJson: suspend () -> String,
    onRestoreBackupJson: suspend (String) -> Result<Unit>,
    onGetAiPrompt: suspend () -> String,
    onClearAllData: () -> Unit,
    onOpenOnboarding: () -> Unit,
    onUpdateProfilePhoto: (String) -> Unit = {},
    onUpdateSoundSettings: (Boolean, Float, Float, Float) -> Unit = { _, _, _, _ -> },
    onSetGithubRepo: (String) -> Unit = {},
    onUpdateGlassSettings: (
        material: String,
        wallpaper: String,
        glassLists: Boolean,
        shrinkTabBar: Boolean,
        motionHighlights: Boolean,
        reduceTransparency: Boolean
    ) -> Unit = { _, _, _, _, _, _ -> }
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    var currentSubScreen by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) } // null, "appearance", "reminders"

    var showClearDataConfirm by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showAboutDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showAiExportDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var aiPromptContent by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var showRestoreDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showBackupSuccessDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var lastSavedBackupResult by remember { mutableStateOf<BackupSaveResult?>(null) }
    var showUpdateDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    // Permission launcher for Android 13+ notifications & background reliability checks
    var hasNotifPermission by remember { mutableStateOf(NotificationHelper.hasNotificationPermission(context)) }
    var canExactAlarm by remember { mutableStateOf(ReminderScheduler.canScheduleExactAlarms(context)) }
    var isIgnoringBattery by remember { mutableStateOf(ReminderScheduler.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(context)) }

    val lifecycleOwner = LocalLifecycleOwner.current
    DisposableEffect(lifecycleOwner) {
        val observer = LifecycleEventObserver { _, event ->
            if (event == Lifecycle.Event.ON_RESUME) {
                hasNotifPermission = NotificationHelper.hasNotificationPermission(context)
                canExactAlarm = ReminderScheduler.canScheduleExactAlarms(context)
                isIgnoringBattery = ReminderScheduler.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(context)
            }
        }
        lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.addObserver(observer)
        onDispose { lifecycleOwner.lifecycle.removeObserver(observer) }
    }

    val permissionLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission()
    ) { granted ->
        hasNotifPermission = granted
        if (granted) {
            Toast.makeText(context, "مجوز اعلان با موفقیت فعال شد.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        } else {
            Toast.makeText(context, "بدون مجوز اعلان، یادآورهای روزانه نمایش داده نخواهند شد.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    val performBackupExport: () -> Unit = {
        coroutineScope.launch {
            try {
                val json = onExportBackupJson()
                val res = BackupHelper.saveBackupWithDetails(context, json)
                lastSavedBackupResult = res
                showBackupSuccessDialog = true
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Toast.makeText(context, "خطا در ایجاد فایل پشتیبان: ${e.message ?: "نامشخص"}", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
            }
        }
    }

    // Legacy storage permission launcher (Android 9 and below only)
    var pendingStorageAction by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) } // "BACKUP" or "RESTORE"
    val legacyStoragePermissionLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()
    ) { grants ->
        val allGranted = grants.values.all { it }
        when (pendingStorageAction) {
            "BACKUP" -> {
                if (!allGranted) {
                    Toast.makeText(context, "مجوز حافظه داده نشد؛ فایل در پوشه اختصاصی برنامه ذخیره شد.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
                performBackupExport()
            }
            "RESTORE" -> showRestoreDialog = true
        }
        pendingStorageAction = null
    }

    // SAF OpenDocument launcher to restore any .json file without broad storage permissions
    val restoreDocumentLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()
    ) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            coroutineScope.launch {
                val readRes = BackupHelper.readBackupFromUri(context, uri)
                if (readRes.isSuccess) {
                    val container = readRes.getOrNull()!!
                    val json = BackupHelper.createBackupJson(
                        settings = container.settings,
                        plans = container.plans,
                        exercises = container.exercises,
                        histories = container.histories,
                        skips = container.skips,
                        supplements = container.supplements,
                        supplementHistories = container.supplementHistories,
                        nutritionLogs = container.nutritionLogs,
                        measurements = container.measurements
                    )
                    val restoreRes = onRestoreBackupJson(json)
                    if (restoreRes.isSuccess) {
                        showRestoreDialog = false
                        Toast.makeText(context, "اطلاعات با موفقیت از فایل انتخاب‌شده بازیابی شد!", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(context, "خطا در بازیابی اطلاعات دیتابیس.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    }
                } else {
                    Toast.makeText(context, "فایل انتخاب‌شده یک پشتیبان معتبر GYM نیست.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // SAF CreateDocument launcher so user can export backup .json to any folder they choose
    val createBackupDocumentLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument("application/json")
    ) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            coroutineScope.launch {
                try {
                    val json = onExportBackupJson()
                    val writeRes = BackupHelper.writeBackupToUri(context, uri, json)
                    if (writeRes.isSuccess) {
                        Toast.makeText(context, "فایل پشتیبان با موفقیت در مسیر انتخابی ذخیره شد.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    } else {
                        Toast.makeText(context, "خطا در ذخیره فایل در مسیر انتخابی.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    }
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Toast.makeText(context, "خطا در ذخیره‌سازی: ${e.message ?: "نامشخص"}", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    var timePickerTarget by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) } // "WORKOUT", "WATER", "SUPPLEMENT"

    val avatarPickerLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()
    ) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            val permanentPath = ImageStorageHelper.saveProfilePhotoPermanently(context, uri)
            onUpdateProfilePhoto(permanentPath)
            Toast.makeText(context, "عکس پروفایل با موفقیت بروز شد.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    // Intercept back button for sub-screens
    BackHandler(enabled = currentSubScreen != null) {
        currentSubScreen = null
    }

    when (currentSubScreen) {
        "appearance" -> {
            // ==========================================
            // زیرصفحه تم و ظاهر Liquid Glass
            // ==========================================
            val currentMaterial = GlassMaterial.fromId(userSettings.glassMaterial)
            val currentWallpaper = GlassWallpaper.fromId(userSettings.glassWallpaper)
            val effectiveDark = LiquidTheme.isDark

            fun saveGlass(
                material: GlassMaterial = currentMaterial,
                wallpaper: GlassWallpaper = currentWallpaper,
                glassLists: Boolean = userSettings.glassLists,
                shrinkTabBar: Boolean = userSettings.shrinkTabBar,
                motionHighlights: Boolean = userSettings.motionHighlights,
                reduceTransparency: Boolean = userSettings.reduceTransparency
            ) {
                onUpdateGlassSettings(
                    material.id,
                    wallpaper.id,
                    glassLists,
                    shrinkTabBar,
                    motionHighlights,
                    reduceTransparency
                )
            }

            Scaffold(
                containerColor = Color.Transparent,
                contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0, 0, 0, 0),
                topBar = {
                    LiquidTopBar(
                        title = "شخصی‌سازی ظاهر",
                        navigationIcon = {
                            LiquidCircleButton(
                                icon = Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                                contentDescription = "بازگشت",
                                onClick = { currentSubScreen = null }
                            )
                        }
                    )
                }
            ) { padding ->
                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(padding)
                        .testTag("appearance_subscreen"),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(
                        start = 16.dp,
                        end = 16.dp,
                        top = 12.dp,
                        bottom = LocalLiquidBottomClearance.current + 24.dp
                    ),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)
                ) {
                    // 1. Appearance Wireframe Preview + Auto System Switch
                    item {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "حالت نمایش")
                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .selectableGroup()
                                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 18.dp),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(44.dp, Alignment.CenterHorizontally)
                                ) {
                                    LookPreviewCard(
                                        label = "روشن",
                                        selected = !effectiveDark,
                                        darkPreview = false,
                                        onClick = {
                                            onSetAppearanceAndPalette("LIGHT", userSettings.accentPalette)
                                        },
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("look_choice_light")
                                    )
                                    LookPreviewCard(
                                        label = "تاریک",
                                        selected = effectiveDark,
                                        darkPreview = true,
                                        onClick = {
                                            onSetAppearanceAndPalette("DARK", userSettings.accentPalette)
                                        },
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("look_choice_dark")
                                    )
                                }
                                HorizontalDivider(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                                    thickness = 0.6.dp,
                                    color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor
                                )
                                GroupedToggleRow(
                                    title = "تنظیم خودکار بر اساس سیستم",
                                    subtitle = "هماهنگ با تم روز و شب گوشی",
                                    checked = userSettings.appearanceMode.equals("SYSTEM", ignoreCase = true),
                                    onCheckedChange = { auto ->
                                        val nextMode = if (auto) "SYSTEM" else if (effectiveDark) "DARK" else "LIGHT"
                                        onSetAppearanceAndPalette(nextMode, userSettings.accentPalette)
                                    },
                                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("switch_appearance_system")
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // 2. Glass Material (Clear vs Tinted)
                    item {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "جنس شیشه (Liquid Glass)")
                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .selectableGroup()
                                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 18.dp),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(20.dp, Alignment.CenterHorizontally)
                                ) {
                                    MaterialPreviewCard(
                                        kind = GlassMaterial.CLEAR,
                                        label = "شفاف و بلوری",
                                        selected = currentMaterial == GlassMaterial.CLEAR,
                                        onClick = { saveGlass(material = GlassMaterial.CLEAR) },
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("material_choice_clear")
                                    )
                                    MaterialPreviewCard(
                                        kind = GlassMaterial.TINTED,
                                        label = "مات و خوانا",
                                        selected = currentMaterial == GlassMaterial.TINTED,
                                        onClick = { saveGlass(material = GlassMaterial.TINTED) },
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("material_choice_tinted")
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            GlassFooterNote(
                                "حالت شفاف جلوه شکست نور و عمق بیشتری دارد؛ حالت مات کنتراست و خوانایی متن‌ها را افزایش می‌دهد."
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // 3. Wallpaper Presets
                    item {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "پس‌زمینه شیشه‌ای")
                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                Column(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .selectableGroup()
                                        .padding(14.dp),
                                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                                ) {
                                    val wallpapers = GlassWallpaper.entries
                                    for (i in wallpapers.indices step 3) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                                        ) {
                                            for (j in 0 until 3) {
                                                val wp = wallpapers.getOrNull(i + j)
                                                if (wp != null) {
                                                    WallpaperPreviewCard(
                                                        wallpaper = wp,
                                                        isDark = effectiveDark,
                                                        palette = LiquidTheme.palette,
                                                        selected = currentWallpaper == wp,

                                                            .weight(1f)

                                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // 4. 8 Accent Palettes (now with برنز قهرمانی & تیتانیوم نقره‌ای)
                    item {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)) {
                            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "پالت‌های رنگی Liquid Glass")
                            val palettes = LiquidPalettes.all
                            for (i in palettes.indices step 2) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                                ) {
                                    val p1 = palettes[i]
                                    val p2 = palettes.getOrNull(i + 1)

                                    Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                        PaletteCard(
                                            palette = p1,
                                            isSelected = userSettings.accentPalette.equals(p1.id, ignoreCase = true),
                                            onClick = {
                                                onSetAppearanceAndPalette(userSettings.appearanceMode, p1.id)
                                            }
                                        )
                                    }

                                    if (p2 != null) {
                                        Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                            PaletteCard(
                                                palette = p2,
                                                isSelected = userSettings.accentPalette.equals(p2.id, ignoreCase = true),
                                                onClick = {
                                                    onSetAppearanceAndPalette(userSettings.appearanceMode, p2.id)
                                                }
                                            )
                                        }
                                    } else {
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // 5. Glass Effects & Motion Toggles
                    item {
                        Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                            LiquidSectionHeader(title = "جلوه‌ها و رفتار شیشه")
                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                GroupedToggleRow(
                                    title = "لیست‌ها و کارت‌های شیشه‌ای",
                                    subtitle = "نمایش کارت‌ها با بافت شیشه‌ای نیمه‌شفاف",
                                    checked = userSettings.glassLists,
                                    enabled = !userSettings.reduceTransparency,
                                    onCheckedChange = { saveGlass(glassLists = it) },
                                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("switch_glass_lists")
                                )
                                HorizontalDivider(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                                    thickness = 0.6.dp,
                                    color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor
                                )
                                GroupedToggleRow(
                                    title = "جمع‌شدن نوار پایین هنگام اسکرول",
                                    subtitle = "تبدیل نوار پایین به کپسول کوچک برای فضای بیشتر",
                                    checked = userSettings.shrinkTabBar,
                                    onCheckedChange = { saveGlass(shrinkTabBar = it) },
                                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("switch_shrink_tab_bar")
                                )
                                HorizontalDivider(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                                    thickness = 0.6.dp,
                                    color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor
                                )
                                GroupedToggleRow(
                                    title = "بازتاب نور با چرخش گوشی",
                                    subtitle = "تغییر زاویه درخشش لبه‌های شیشه با سنسور حرکت",
                                    checked = userSettings.motionHighlights,
                                    enabled = !userSettings.reduceTransparency,
                                    onCheckedChange = { saveGlass(motionHighlights = it) },
                                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("switch_motion_highlights")
                                )
                                HorizontalDivider(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
                                    thickness = 0.6.dp,
                                    color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor
                                )
                                GroupedToggleRow(
                                    title = "کاهش شفافیت (خوانایی بیشتر)",
                                    subtitle = "جایگزینی سطوح مات و حذف محوشدگی پس‌زمینه",
                                    checked = userSettings.reduceTransparency,
                                    onCheckedChange = { saveGlass(reduceTransparency = it) },
                                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("switch_reduce_transparency")
                                )
                            }
                            GlassFooterNote(
                                "محوشدگی زنده در اندروید ۱۲ به بالا و شکست نور نوری (عدسی Liquid Glass) در اندروید ۱۳ به بالا فعال می‌شود."
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        "reminders" -> {
            // ==========================================
            // زیرصفحه یادآورها و اعلان‌ها
            // ==========================================
            Scaffold(
                containerColor = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.Transparent,
                contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0, 0, 0, 0),
                topBar = {
                    LiquidTopBar(
                        title = "تنظیم یادآورها",
                        navigationIcon = {
                            LiquidCircleButton(
                                icon = Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                                contentDescription = "بازگشت",
                                onClick = { currentSubScreen = null }
                            )
                        }
                    )
                }
            ) { padding ->
                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(padding),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(
                        start = 16.dp,
                        end = 16.dp,
                        top = 12.dp,
                        bottom = LocalLiquidBottomClearance.current + 20.dp
                    ),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
                ) {
                    // Permission Banner if not granted
                    if (!hasNotifPermission) {
                        item {
                            Surface(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                                color = SemanticWarning.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                            ) {
                                Column(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(14.dp),
                                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(Icons.Filled.NotificationsOff, contentDescription = null, tint = SemanticWarning)
                                        Text(
                                            "مجوز اعلان‌ها غیرفعال است",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            color = SemanticWarning
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Text(
                                        "برای دریافت هشدارهای روزانه تمرین و آب، لطفاً دسترسی به اعلان را فعال کنید.",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                    )

                                        text = "فعال‌سازی مجوز اعلان",
                                        onClick = {

                                        }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }

                    // Exact Alarm Permission Banner (Android 12+) if revoked
                    if (!canExactAlarm) {

                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                Column(

                                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                    ) {

                                        Text(
                                            "دسترسی زنگ دقیق غیرفعال است",

                                        )
                                    }

                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                    )

                                        text = "فعال‌سازی آلارم دقیق",
                                        onClick = { ReminderScheduler.openExactAlarmSettings(context) }
                                    )
                                }

                        }
                    }

                    // Battery Optimization Banner for closed-app delivery reliability
                    if (!isIgnoringBattery) {
                        item {
                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                Column(
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(14.dp),
                                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(Icons.Filled.BatteryAlert, contentDescription = null, tint = LiquidTheme.iconOrange)
                                        Text(
                                            "جلوگیری از توقف یادآور در پس‌زمینه",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Text(
                                        "در برخی گوشی‌ها (مثل سامسونگ و شیائومی)، بهینه‌ساز باتری باعث می‌شود یادآورها زمانی که برنامه بسته است با تاخیر یا بعد از باز کردن برنامه یکجا بیایند. در صفحه بازشده، GYM PRO را روی «نامحدود / Unrestricted» بگذارید.",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                        color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                    )
                                    LiquidSecondaryButton(
                                        text = "تنظیم باتری در حالت نامحدود",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.Settings,
                                        onClick = { ReminderScheduler.openBatteryOptimizationSettings(context) }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    item {
                        LiquidSectionHeader(title = "هشدارهای روزانه")

                        SolidGroupedCard {
                            GroupedRow(
                                title = "یادآور تمرین ورزشی",
                                subtitle = "ساعت: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(userSettings.workoutReminderTime)} (برای تغییر ساعت لمس کنید)",
                                icon = Icons.Filled.FitnessCenter,
                                iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconRed,
                                showArrow = true,
                                showDivider = true,
                                onClick = { timePickerTarget = "WORKOUT" },
                                trailingContent = {
                                    LiquidSwitch(
                                        checked = userSettings.workoutReminderEnabled,
                                        onCheckedChange = { en ->
                                            if (en && !hasNotifPermission) {
                                                permissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
                                            }
                                            onToggleReminder("WORKOUT", en, userSettings.workoutReminderTime)
                                        }
                                    )
                                }
                            )

                            GroupedRow(
                                title = "یادآور نوشیدن آب",
                                subtitle = "ساعت: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(userSettings.waterReminderTime)} (برای تغییر ساعت لمس کنید)",
                                icon = Icons.Filled.WaterDrop,
                                iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconCyan,
                                showArrow = true,
                                showDivider = true,
                                onClick = { timePickerTarget = "WATER" },
                                trailingContent = {
                                    LiquidSwitch(
                                        checked = userSettings.waterReminderEnabled,
                                        onCheckedChange = { en ->
                                            if (en && !hasNotifPermission) {
                                                permissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
                                            }
                                            onToggleReminder("WATER", en, userSettings.waterReminderTime)
                                        }
                                    )
                                }
                            )

                            GroupedRow(
                                title = "یادآور مصرف مکمل‌ها",
                                subtitle = "ساعت: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(userSettings.supplementReminderTime)} (برای تغییر ساعت لمس کنید)",
                                icon = Icons.Filled.Medication,
                                iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconGreen,
                                showArrow = true,
                                showDivider = false,
                                onClick = { timePickerTarget = "SUPPLEMENT" },
                                trailingContent = {
                                    LiquidSwitch(
                                        checked = userSettings.supplementReminderEnabled,
                                        onCheckedChange = { en ->
                                            if (en && !hasNotifPermission) {
                                                permissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
                                            }
                                            onToggleReminder("SUPPLEMENT", en, userSettings.supplementReminderTime)
                                        }
                                    )
                                }
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    // In-app Sound Settings with individual sliders
                    item {
                        LiquidSectionHeader(title = "صداها و هشدارهای داخل برنامه")

                        SolidGroupedCard {
                            Column(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(16.dp),
                                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
                            ) {
                                // 1. Global in-app sounds toggle
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Row(
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                                    ) {
                                        Box(
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .size(36.dp)
                                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                                .background(LiquidTheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.16f)),
                                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                        ) {
                                            Icon(
                                                imageVector = if (userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled) Icons.Filled.VolumeUp else Icons.Filled.VolumeOff,
                                                contentDescription = null,
                                                tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                            )
                                        }
                                        Column {
                                            Text(
                                                "صداهای داخل برنامه",
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                                                color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                            )
                                            Text(
                                                "بوق تایمر استراحت و آلارم حرکات زمان‌دار",
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                                color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }

                                    LiquidSwitch(
                                        checked = userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled,
                                        onCheckedChange = { en ->
                                            onUpdateSoundSettings(
                                                en,
                                                userSettings.restTimerVolume,
                                                userSettings.timedAlarmVolume,
                                                userSettings.reminderVolume
                                            )
                                        }
                                    )
                                }

                                if (userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled) {
                                    HorizontalDivider(color = LiquidTheme.dividerColor, thickness = 0.5.dp)

                                    var restVolDraft by remember(userSettings.restTimerVolume) {
                                        mutableFloatStateOf(userSettings.restTimerVolume)
                                    }
                                    var timedVolDraft by remember(userSettings.timedAlarmVolume) {
                                        mutableFloatStateOf(userSettings.timedAlarmVolume)
                                    }
                                    var reminderVolDraft by remember(userSettings.reminderVolume) {
                                        mutableFloatStateOf(userSettings.reminderVolume)
                                    }

                                    // 2. Rest timer beep volume slider
                                    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Text("حجم بوق تایمر استراحت:", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary)
                                            Text("${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits((restVolDraft * 100).toInt())}٪", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LiquidTheme.accentColor)
                                        }
                                        Slider(
                                            value = restVolDraft,
                                            onValueChange = { newVol ->
                                                restVolDraft = newVol
                                                SoundPrefsCache.save(context, userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled, newVol, timedVolDraft, reminderVolDraft)
                                            },
                                            onValueChangeFinished = {
                                                onUpdateSoundSettings(
                                                    userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled,
                                                    restVolDraft,
                                                    timedVolDraft,
                                                    reminderVolDraft
                                                )
                                                NotificationHelper.playAlarmSoundAndVibrate(
                                                    context,
                                                    AlertType.REST_END,
                                                    overrideVolume = restVolDraft,
                                                    withVibration = false
                                                )
                                            },
                                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("slider_rest_volume"),
                                            colors = SliderDefaults.colors(
                                                thumbColor = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                activeTrackColor = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                inactiveTrackColor = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                            )
                                        )
                                    }

                                    // 3. Timed exercise alarm volume slider
                                    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Text("حجم آلارم پایان حرکات زمان‌دار (پلانک):", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary)
                                            Text("${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits((timedVolDraft * 100).toInt())}٪", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LiquidTheme.accentColor)
                                        }
                                        Slider(
                                            value = timedVolDraft,
                                            onValueChange = { newVol ->
                                                timedVolDraft = newVol
                                                SoundPrefsCache.save(context, userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled, restVolDraft, newVol, reminderVolDraft)
                                            },
                                            onValueChangeFinished = {
                                                onUpdateSoundSettings(
                                                    userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled,
                                                    restVolDraft,
                                                    timedVolDraft,
                                                    reminderVolDraft
                                                )
                                                NotificationHelper.playAlarmSoundAndVibrate(
                                                    context,
                                                    AlertType.EXERCISE_END,
                                                    overrideVolume = timedVolDraft,
                                                    withVibration = false
                                                )
                                            },
                                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("slider_timed_volume"),
                                            colors = SliderDefaults.colors(
                                                thumbColor = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                activeTrackColor = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                inactiveTrackColor = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                            )
                                        )
                                    }

                                    // 4. Reminder notification volume slider
                                    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                        ) {
                                            Text("حجم صدای زنگ اعلان‌ها و یادآورها:", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary)
                                            Text("${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits((reminderVolDraft * 100).toInt())}٪", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LiquidTheme.accentColor)
                                        }
                                        Slider(
                                            value = reminderVolDraft,
                                            onValueChange = { newVol ->
                                                reminderVolDraft = newVol
                                                SoundPrefsCache.save(context, userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled, restVolDraft, timedVolDraft, newVol)
                                            },
                                            onValueChangeFinished = {
                                                onUpdateSoundSettings(
                                                    userSettings.inAppSoundsEnabled,
                                                    restVolDraft,
                                                    timedVolDraft,
                                                    reminderVolDraft
                                                )
                                                NotificationHelper.playAlarmSoundAndVibrate(
                                                    context,
                                                    AlertType.WORKOUT,
                                                    overrideVolume = reminderVolDraft,
                                                    withVibration = false
                                                )
                                            },
                                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("slider_reminder_volume"),
                                            colors = SliderDefaults.colors(
                                                thumbColor = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                activeTrackColor = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                inactiveTrackColor = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                            )
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    item {
                        LiquidPrimaryButton(
                            text = "تست ارسال اعلان و آلارم صوتی",
                            icon = Icons.Filled.NotificationsActive,
                            onClick = {
                                if (!hasNotifPermission && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
                                    permissionLauncher.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
                                }
                                onTestNotification()
                                Toast.makeText(context, "اعلان و زنگ تست با موفقیت ارسال شد!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        else -> {
            // ==========================================
            // صفحه اصلی تنظیمات (Main Settings)
            // ==========================================
            Scaffold(
                containerColor = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color.Transparent,
                contentWindowInsets = WindowInsets(0, 0, 0, 0),
                topBar = {
                    LiquidTopBar(
                        title = "تنظیمات و پروفایل",
                        actions = {
                            LiquidCircleButton(
                                icon = Icons.Filled.Edit,
                                contentDescription = "ویرایش مشخصات",
                                onClick = onOpenOnboarding
                            )
                        }
                    )
                }
            ) { padding ->
                LazyColumn(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxSize()
                        .padding(padding)
                        .testTag("profile_screen"),
                    contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = LocalLiquidBottomClearance.current + 24.dp),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
                ) {
                    // Profile Header Card
                    item {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .clickable { onOpenOnboarding() }
                                        .padding(16.dp),
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Box(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .size(68.dp)
                                            .clip(CircleShape)
                                            .background(LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated)
                                            .clickable { avatarPickerLauncher.launch("image/*") },
                                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                    ) {
                                        if (userSettings.profilePhotoUri.isNotBlank()) {
                                            AsyncImage(
                                                model = userSettings.profilePhotoUri,
                                                contentDescription = "عکس پروفایل",
                                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                                                contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                                            )
                                        } else {
                                            Icon(
                                                imageVector = Icons.Filled.Person,
                                                contentDescription = null,
                                                tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(36.dp)
                                            )
                                        }

                                        // Camera edit badge
                                        Box(
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .align(Alignment.BottomEnd)
                                                .size(22.dp)
                                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                                .background(LiquidTheme.accentColor),
                                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                        ) {
                                            Icon(
                                                imageVector = Icons.Filled.CameraAlt,
                                                contentDescription = "تغییر عکس",
                                                tint = LiquidTheme.onAccent,
                                                modifier = Modifier.size(12.dp)
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }

                                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                        Text(
