                                            text = if (userSettings.userName.isNotBlank()) userSettings.userName else "ورزشکار حرفه‌ای",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                        )
                                        Text(
                                            text = "هدف: ${
                                                when (userSettings.fitnessGoal) {
                                                    "BULK" -> "افزایش حجم عضلانی"
                                                    "CUT" -> "کاهش چربی و تفکیک"
                                                    else -> "حفظ تناسب و سلامت عمومی"
                                                }
                                            }",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                        )
                                    }

                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowForward,
                                        contentDescription = null,
                                        tint = LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Group 1: Appearance & Reminders
                    item {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                LiquidSectionHeader(title = "تنظیمات عمومی")

                                SolidGroupedCard {
                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "ظاهر و پالت‌های رنگی",
                                        subtitle = "حالت نمایش، جنس شیشه، پس‌زمینه و پالت رنگی",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.Palette,
                                        iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconPurple,
                                        showDivider = true,
                                        onClick = { currentSubScreen = "appearance" }
                                    )

                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "یادآورها و هشدارها",
                                        subtitle = "تمرین، مصرف آب و مکمل‌ها",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.Notifications,
                                        iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconOrange,
                                        showDivider = false,
                                        onClick = { currentSubScreen = "reminders" }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Group 2: Dedicated File Storage Backup, Restore & Updates
                    item {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                LiquidSectionHeader(title = "پشتیبان‌گیری و بروزرسانی")

                                SolidGroupedCard {
                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "ایجاد فایل پشتیبان جدید در حافظه",
                                        subtitle = "ذخیره فایل JSON در پوشه دانلودها و حافظه برنامه",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.Save,
                                        iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconBlue,
                                        showDivider = true,
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("create_backup_file_row"),
                                        onClick = {
                                            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.Q && !BackupHelper.hasLegacyStoragePermission(context)) {
                                                pendingStorageAction = "BACKUP"
                                                legacyStoragePermissionLauncher.launch(
                                                    arrayOf(
                                                        Manifest.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE,
                                                        Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            } else {
                                                performBackupExport()
                                            }
                                        }
                                    )

                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "ذخیره فایل پشتیبان در مسیر دلخواه",
                                        subtitle = "انتخاب پوشه دلخواه با مدیریت فایل سیستم",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.CreateNewFolder,
                                        iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconPurple,
                                        showDivider = true,
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("save_backup_saf_row"),
                                        onClick = {
                                            try {
                                                createBackupDocumentLauncher.launch("gym_pro_backup.json")
                                            } catch (_: Exception) {
                                                Toast.makeText(context, "امکان باز کردن انتخابگر پوشه وجود ندارد.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                            }
                                        }
                                    )

                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "بازیابی اطلاعات از فایل پشتیبان",
                                        subtitle = "بازیابی از پوشه برنامه یا انتخاب فایل از گوشی",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.Restore,
                                        iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconGreen,
                                        showDivider = true,
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("restore_backup_file_row"),
                                        onClick = {
                                            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.Q && !BackupHelper.hasLegacyStoragePermission(context)) {
                                                pendingStorageAction = "RESTORE"
                                                legacyStoragePermissionLauncher.launch(
                                                    arrayOf(
                                                        Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE,
                                                        Manifest.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            } else {
                                                showRestoreDialog = true
                                            }
                                        }
                                    )

                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "بروزرسانی برنامه",
                                        subtitle = "نسخه فعلی: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME)} • بررسی و دانلود نسخه جدید",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.SystemUpdate,
                                        iconTint = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                        showDivider = true,
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("check_update_row"),
                                        onClick = { showUpdateDialog = true }
                                    )

                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "خروجی هوشمند برای هوش مصنوعی",
                                        subtitle = "پرامپت آنالیز جامع تمرین و تغذیه",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.AutoAwesome,
                                        iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconIndigo,
                                        showDivider = true,
                                        onClick = {
                                            coroutineScope.launch {
                                                aiPromptContent = onGetAiPrompt()
                                                showAiExportDialog = true
                                            }
                                        }
                                    )

                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "درباره GYM",
                                        subtitle = "انگیزشی • ارتباط با توسعه‌دهنده",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.Info,
                                        iconTint = LiquidTheme.iconCyan,
                                        showDivider = false,
                                        onClick = { showAboutDialog = true }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Group 3: Destructive Group (Clear All Data in red)
                    item {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                                LiquidSectionHeader(title = "منطقه حساس")

                                SolidGroupedCard {
                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = "حذف کامل تمامی اطلاعات",
                                        subtitle = "پاک کردن تمامی برنامه‌ها، سوابق و تنظیمات",
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.DeleteForever,
                                        iconTint = SemanticError,
                                        showArrow = false,
                                        showDivider = false,
                                        onClick = { showClearDataConfirm = true }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Backup Created Success Dialog
    if (showBackupSuccessDialog) {
        val result = lastSavedBackupResult
        val file = result?.appFile ?: BackupHelper.getAppBackupFile(context)
        val sizeKb = (file.length() / 1024).coerceAtLeast(1)
        val displayLoc = result?.displayLocation ?: "پوشه اختصاصی GymPro_Backups"
        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showBackupSuccessDialog = false },
            title = "فایل پشتیبان ذخیره شد",
            message = if (result?.exportedToDownloads == true) {
                "فایل پشتیبان با موفقیت در پوشه دانلودها و حافظه اختصاصی برنامه ذخیره شد."
            } else {
                "فایل پشتیبان در پوشه اختصاصی برنامه ذخیره شد. همچنین می‌توانید آن را به صورت مستقیم به اشتراک بگذارید."
            },
            confirmText = "ارسال و اشتراک فایل",
            dismissText = "بستن",
            onConfirm = {
                try {
                    val shareUri = BackupHelper.getShareUri(context, file)
                    val shareIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                        type = "application/json"
                        putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, "فایل پشتیبان GYM PRO")
                        putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, shareUri)
                        addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                    }
                    context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(shareIntent, "اشتراک‌گذاری فایل پشتیبان"))
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    Toast.makeText(context, "فایل در مسیر $displayLoc ذخیره شده است.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            },
            content = {
                Surface(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                    color = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                ) {
                    Column(
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp),
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text("نام فایل: ${file.name}", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary)
                        Text("حجم فایل: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(sizeKb)} کیلوبایت", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary)
                        Text("مسیر: $displayLoc", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall, color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary)
                    }
                }
            }
        )
    }

    // Restore from File Dialog
    if (showRestoreDialog) {
        val backupFiles = remember(showRestoreDialog) {
            BackupHelper.listBackupFilesSafely(context)
        }

        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showRestoreDialog = false },
            title = "بازیابی از فایل پشتیبان",
            message = if (backupFiles.isNotEmpty()) "فایل موردنظر را از لیست زیر انتخاب کنید یا فایل JSON را از حافظه گوشی برگزینید:" else "فایل پشتیبان خود را از حافظه گوشی انتخاب کنید:",
            confirmText = "انتخاب فایل از حافظه",
            dismissText = "انصراف",
            onConfirm = {
                try {
                    restoreDocumentLauncher.launch(arrayOf("application/json", "text/plain", "*/*"))
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    Toast.makeText(context, "برنامه مدیریت فایلی برای انتخاب فایل در دسترس نیست.", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                }
            },
            content = {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                ) {
                    if (backupFiles.isNotEmpty()) {
                        Text(
                            text = "فایل‌های آماده در حافظه برنامه:",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                        )
                        backupFiles.take(5).forEach { file ->
                            val sizeKb = (file.length() / 1024).coerceAtLeast(1)
                            val modifiedDate = java.text.SimpleDateFormat("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm", java.util.Locale.US).format(java.util.Date(file.lastModified()))
                            Surface(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .liquidTapEffect {
                                        coroutineScope.launch {
                                            val readRes = BackupHelper.readBackupFromFile(file)
                                            if (readRes.isSuccess) {
                                                val c = readRes.getOrNull()!!
                                                val json = BackupHelper.createBackupJson(
                                                    settings = c.settings,
                                                    plans = c.plans,
                                                    exercises = c.exercises,
                                                    histories = c.histories,
                                                    skips = c.skips,
                                                    supplements = c.supplements,
                                                    supplementHistories = c.supplementHistories,
                                                    nutritionLogs = c.nutritionLogs,
                                                    measurements = c.measurements
                                                )
                                                val res = onRestoreBackupJson(json)
                                                if (res.isSuccess) {
                                                    showRestoreDialog = false
                                                    Toast.makeText(context, "اطلاعات با موفقیت از فایل «${file.name}» بازیابی شد!", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                                                } else {
                                                    Toast.makeText(context, "خطا در واردسازی دیتابیس.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                                }
                                            } else {
                                                Toast.makeText(context, "فایل پشتیبان نامعتبر است.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                            }
                                        }
                                    },
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                                color = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                            ) {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .padding(12.dp),
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Box(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .size(34.dp)
                                            .clip(CircleShape)
                                            .background(LiquidTheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.16f)),
                                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(
                                            Icons.Filled.RestorePage,
                                            contentDescription = null,
                                            tint = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                                        )
                                    }
                                    Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = file.name,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                        )
                                        Text(
                                            text = "تاریخ: $modifiedDate • حجم: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(sizeKb)} KB",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        )
    }

    // AI Prompt Export Dialog
    if (showAiExportDialog) {
        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showAiExportDialog = false },
            title = "پرامپت هوشمند تحلیل ورزشی",
            message = "این متن شامل داده‌های بدنی و تمرینی شما برای ارائه به هوش مصنوعی است:",
            confirmText = "کپی در کلیپ‌بورد",
            dismissText = "بستن",
            onConfirm = {
                val clipboard = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager
                clipboard.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText("Gym AI Prompt", aiPromptContent))
                Toast.makeText(context, "در کلیپ‌بورد کپی شد!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            },
            content = {
                LiquidInputField(
                    value = aiPromptContent,
                    onValueChange = {},
                    label = "متن پرامپت",
                    singleLine = false,
                    modifier = Modifier.height(140.dp)
                )
            }
        )
    }

    // Clear All Confirmation Dialog
    if (showClearDataConfirm) {
        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showClearDataConfirm = false },
            title = "تأیید حذف کامل داده‌ها",
            message = "آیا مطمئن هستید؟ با این کار تمامی برنامه‌ها، سوابق جلسات، ثبت‌های تغذیه، اندازه‌های بدن و تنظیمات برای همیشه حذف خواهند شد.",
            confirmText = "بله، همه را حذف کن",
            dismissText = "انصراف",
            isDestructive = true,
            onConfirm = {
                onClearAllData()
                Toast.makeText(context, "تمامی داده‌ها با موفقیت پاک شدند.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        )
    }

    // About Dialog with Motivational Bodybuilding Quote & Clickable Telegram / Instagram Links
    if (showAboutDialog) {
        LiquidAlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showAboutDialog = false },
            title = "درباره GYM",
            message = "«بدن شما تنها خانه‌ای است که تا پایان عمر در آن زندگی می‌کنید. هر قطره عرق، هر ست سنگین و هر ثانیه‌ای که تسلیم نشدید، ساختن نسخه‌ای قوی‌تر، شجاع‌تر و متعهدتر از خودِ شماست. درد تمرین موقتی است، اما افتخار ساختن قهرمان درونتان تا ابد باقی می‌ماند!»",
            confirmText = "بستن",
            onConfirm = {},
            content = {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "ارتباط با توسعه‌دهنده و سازنده:",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                    )

                    // Telegram Button Card
                    Surface(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .liquidTapEffect {
                                try {
                                    val tgIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://t.me/momo1f"))
                                    context.startActivity(tgIntent)
                                } catch (_: Exception) {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "خطا در باز کردن تلگرام", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            },
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                        color = BrandTelegram.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                painter = painterResource(id = R.drawable.ic_telegram),
                                contentDescription = "تلگرام توسعه‌دهنده",
                                tint = Color.Unspecified,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "تلگرام توسعه‌دهنده",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary,
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            )
                            Icon(Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowForward, contentDescription = null, tint = LiquidTheme.textSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                        }
                    }

                    // Instagram Button Card
                    Surface(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .liquidTapEffect {
                                try {
                                    val igIntent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse("https://instagram.com/die3man"))
                                    context.startActivity(igIntent)
                                } catch (_: Exception) {
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "خطا در باز کردن اینستاگرام", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            },
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                        color = BrandInstagram.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                painter = painterResource(id = R.drawable.ic_instagram),
                                contentDescription = "اینستاگرام توسعه‌دهنده",
                                tint = Color.Unspecified,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "اینستاگرام توسعه‌دهنده",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary,
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            )
                            Icon(Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowForward, contentDescription = null, tint = LiquidTheme.textSecondary, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        )
    }

    if (timePickerTarget != null) {
        val currTarget = timePickerTarget!!
        val currTime = when (currTarget) {
            "WORKOUT" -> userSettings.workoutReminderTime
            "WATER" -> userSettings.waterReminderTime
            else -> userSettings.supplementReminderTime
        }
        val currTitle = when (currTarget) {
            "WORKOUT" -> "ساعت یادآور تمرین"
            "WATER" -> "ساعت یادآور آب"
            else -> "ساعت یادآور مکمل‌ها"
        }
        val isEnabled = when (currTarget) {
            "WORKOUT" -> userSettings.workoutReminderEnabled
            "WATER" -> userSettings.waterReminderEnabled
            else -> userSettings.supplementReminderEnabled
        }
        LiquidTimePickerDialog(
            initialTime = currTime,
            title = currTitle,
            onDismissRequest = { timePickerTarget = null },
            onConfirm = { newTime ->
                onToggleReminder(currTarget, isEnabled, newTime)
                timePickerTarget = null
                Toast.makeText(context, "ساعت اعلان با موفقیت تنظیم شد: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(newTime)}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        )
    }

    if (showUpdateDialog) {
        AppUpdateDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showUpdateDialog = false }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AppUpdateDialog(
    onDismissRequest: () -> Unit
) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val targetRepo = remember { AppUpdateHelper.getEffectiveRepo() }

    var isChecking by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var checkError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var upToDateVersion by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var availableUpdate by remember { mutableStateOf<ReleaseUpdateInfo?>(null) }

    var isDownloading by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var downloadProgress by remember { mutableIntStateOf(0) }
    var downloadedBytes by remember { mutableLongStateOf(0L) }
    var totalBytes by remember { mutableLongStateOf(0L) }
    var downloadedFile by remember { mutableStateOf<File?>(null) }
    var downloadError by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var downloadJob by remember { mutableStateOf<Job?>(null) }

    fun triggerCheck() {
        isChecking = true
        checkError = null
        upToDateVersion = null
        availableUpdate = null
        downloadedFile = null
        downloadError = null

        scope.launch {
            val res = AppUpdateHelper.checkForUpdate(targetRepo)
            isChecking = false
            res.fold(
                onSuccess = { outcome ->
                    when (outcome) {
                        is UpdateCheckResult.UpToDate -> {
                            upToDateVersion = outcome.latestVersion
                        }
                        is UpdateCheckResult.UpdateAvailable -> {
                            availableUpdate = outcome.info
                        }
                    }
                },
                onFailure = { err ->
                    checkError = err.message ?: "خطا در بررسی نسخه جدید."
                }
            )
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        triggerCheck()
    }

    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose {
            downloadJob?.cancel()
        }
    }

    fun startDownloadAndInstall(info: ReleaseUpdateInfo) {
        if (isDownloading) return
        isDownloading = true
        downloadError = null
        downloadProgress = 0
        downloadedBytes = 0L
        totalBytes = info.apkSizeBytes
        downloadJob = scope.launch {
            val res = AppUpdateHelper.downloadApk(
                context = context,
                downloadUrl = info.apkDownloadUrl,
                fileName = info.apkName,
                onProgress = { pct, dl, total ->
                    downloadProgress = pct
                    downloadedBytes = dl
                    totalBytes = total
                }
            )
            isDownloading = false
            res.fold(
                onSuccess = { apk ->
                    downloadedFile = apk
                    when (AppUpdateHelper.installApk(context, apk)) {
                        InstallLaunchStatus.NEEDS_PERMISSION -> {
                            Toast.makeText(
                                context,
                                "دانلود کامل شد! مجوز نصب را فعال کنید و سپس روی «نصب نسخه دانلود شده» بزنید.",
                                Toast.LENGTH_LONG
                            ).show()
                        }
                        InstallLaunchStatus.ERROR -> {
                            downloadError = "فایل دانلود شد اما اجرای نصب با خطا مواجه شد."
                        }
                        InstallLaunchStatus.LAUNCHED -> Unit
                    }
                },
                onFailure = { err ->
                    downloadError = err.message ?: "خطا در دانلود فایل بروزرسانی."
                }
            )
        }
    }

    fun handleInstallReadyApk(apk: File) {
        when (AppUpdateHelper.installApk(context, apk)) {
            InstallLaunchStatus.NEEDS_PERMISSION -> {
                Toast.makeText(
                    context,
                    "لطفاً مجوز نصب برنامه از منابع ناشناس را فعال کرده و سپس دکمه نصب را دوباره بزنید.",
                    Toast.LENGTH_LONG
                ).show()
            }
            InstallLaunchStatus.ERROR -> {
                Toast.makeText(context, "خطا در اجرای نصب‌کننده.", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
            InstallLaunchStatus.LAUNCHED -> Unit
        }
    }

    val closeDialog: () -> Unit = {
        downloadJob?.cancel()
        onDismissRequest()
    }

    val actionLabel: String? = when {
        downloadedFile != null -> "نصب نسخه جدید"
        isDownloading -> null
        availableUpdate != null -> "دانلود و نصب"
        isChecking -> null
        checkError != null || upToDateVersion != null -> "بررسی مجدد"
        else -> "بررسی بروزرسانی"
    }

    Dialog(
        onDismissRequest = {
            if (!isDownloading) closeDialog()
        },
        properties = DialogProperties(usePlatformDefaultWidth = false)
    ) {
        Box(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.75f))
                .clickable(enabled = !isDownloading) { closeDialog() },
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .width(330.dp)
                    .clickable(enabled = false) {}
                    .liquidGlass(
                        useBackdrop = false,
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                        tintAlpha = if (LiquidTheme.isDark) 0.90f else 0.95f,
                        elevation = 16.dp
                    )
            ) {
                Column(
                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                ) {
                    Column(
                        Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(22.dp),
                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "بروزرسانی GYM PRO",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "نسخه فعلی نصب‌شده: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME)}",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                        )

                        Column(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .heightIn(max = 360.dp)
                                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                        ) {
                            when {
                                isChecking -> {
                                    Column(
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxWidth()
                                            .padding(vertical = 12.dp),
                                        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                                        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        LiquidGlassSpinner(size = 54.dp)
                                        Text(
                                            text = "در حال بررسی آخرین نسخه...",
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                            color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                        )
                                    }
                                }

                                availableUpdate != null -> {
                                    val info = availableUpdate!!
                                    Surface(
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                                        color = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                    ) {
                                        Column(
                                            modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp),
                                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                                        ) {
                                            Row(
                                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                            ) {
                                                Text(
                                                    text = "نسخه جدید موجود است!",
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                                    color = SemanticSuccess
                                                )
                                                Surface(
                                                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.18f)
                                                ) {
                                                    Text(
                                                        text = "v${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(info.versionName)}",
                                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                                        color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            }
                                            if (info.apkSizeBytes > 0L) {
                                                val sizeMb = String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.1f", info.apkSizeBytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
                                                Text(
                                                    text = "حجم فایل: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(sizeMb)} مگابایت",
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                                )
                                            }
                                            if (info.changelog.isNotBlank()) {
                                                Text(
                                                    text = info.changelog,
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }

                                    if (isDownloading) {
                                        Column(
                                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                                        ) {
                                            val dlMb = String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.1f", downloadedBytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
                                            val pctText = if (downloadProgress >= 0) {
                                                val totalText = if (totalBytes > 0L) {
                                                    " (${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(dlMb)} / ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.1f", totalBytes / (1024.0 * 1024.0)))} MB)"
                                                } else ""
                                                "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(downloadProgress)}٪$totalText"
                                            } else {
                                                "${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(dlMb)} مگابایت"
                                            }
                                            Row(
                                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                                            ) {
                                                Text(
                                                    "در حال دانلود فایل APK...",
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary
                                                )
                                                Text(
                                                    pctText,
                                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.accentColor
                                                )
                                            }
                                            if (downloadProgress >= 0) {
                                                LinearProgressIndicator(
                                                    progress = { (downloadProgress / 100f).coerceIn(0f, 1f) },
                                                    modifier = Modifier
                                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                                        .height(8.dp)
                                                        .clip(CircleShape),
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                    trackColor = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                                )
                                            } else {
                                                LinearProgressIndicator(
                                                    modifier = Modifier
                                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                                        .height(8.dp)
                                                        .clip(CircleShape),
                                                    color = LiquidTheme.accentColor,
                                                    trackColor = LiquidTheme.surfaceElevated
                                                )
                                            }
                                        }
                                    } else if (downloadedFile != null) {
                                        LiquidPrimaryButton(
                                            text = "نصب نسخه دانلود شده",
                                            icon = Icons.Filled.InstallMobile,
                                            onClick = { handleInstallReadyApk(downloadedFile!!) },
                                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_install_downloaded_apk")
                                        )
                                    } else {
                                        LiquidPrimaryButton(
                                            text = "دانلود و نصب مستقیم",
                                            icon = Icons.Filled.Download,
                                            onClick = { startDownloadAndInstall(info) },
                                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_download_update_apk")
                                        )
                                    }

                                    if (!downloadError.isNullOrBlank()) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = downloadError!!,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                            color = SemanticError
                                        )
                                    }
                                }

                                upToDateVersion != null -> {
                                    Surface(
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                                        color = SemanticSuccess.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                                    ) {
                                        Row(
                                            modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp),
                                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                                        ) {
                                            Icon(Icons.Filled.CheckCircle, contentDescription = null, tint = SemanticSuccess)
                                            Text(
                                                text = "آخرین نسخه نصب است (نسخه ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(upToDateVersion!!)})",
                                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                                                color = LiquidTheme.textPrimary
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }

                                !checkError.isNullOrBlank() -> {
                                    Surface(
                                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                                        color = SemanticError.copy(alpha = 0.14f)
                                    ) {
                                        Text(
                                            text = checkError!!,
                                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                            color = SemanticError,
                                            modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp)
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Box(
                        Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(0.5.dp)
                            .background(LiquidTheme.dividerColor)
                    )

                    Row(
                        Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(54.dp)
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .fillMaxHeight()
                                .testTag("btn_update_dialog_close")
                                .clickable { closeDialog() },
                            Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = if (isDownloading) "انصراف از دانلود" else "بستن",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                color = if (isDownloading) SemanticError else LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium
                            )
                        }

                        if (actionLabel != null) {
                            Box(
                                Modifier
                                    .width(0.5.dp)
                                    .fillMaxHeight()
                                    .background(LiquidTheme.dividerColor)
                            )
                            Box(
                                Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .fillMaxHeight()
                                    .testTag("btn_update_dialog_action")
                                    .clickable {
                                        val readyApk = downloadedFile
                                        val update = availableUpdate
                                        when {
                                            readyApk != null -> handleInstallReadyApk(readyApk)
                                            update != null -> startDownloadAndInstall(update)
                                            else -> triggerCheck()
                                        }
                                    },
                                Alignment.Center
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = actionLabel,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = LiquidTheme.accentColor
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun PreviewChoiceBox(
    label: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    preview: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .defaultMinSize(minWidth = 48.dp, minHeight = 48.dp)
            .selectable(
                selected = selected,
                interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                indication = null,
                role = Role.RadioButton,
                onClick = onClick
            ),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
    ) {
        preview()
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
            fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
            color = c.label,
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
        )
        GlassRadioDot(selected = selected)
    }
}

@Composable
private fun GlassRadioDot(selected: Boolean) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .size(22.dp)
            .clip(CircleShape)
            .background(if (selected) c.tint else Color.Transparent)
            .border(1.5.dp, if (selected) c.tint else c.separator, CircleShape),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        if (selected) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = Icons.Filled.Check,
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = onColorFor(c.tint),
                modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp)
            )
        }
    }
}

private fun Modifier.previewChoiceRing(selected: Boolean, c: GlassColors, shape: Shape): Modifier =
    border(if (selected) 2.2.dp else 0.6.dp, if (selected) c.tint else c.separator, shape)

@Composable
private fun LookPreviewCard(
    label: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    darkPreview: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
    PreviewChoiceBox(label = label, selected = selected, onClick = onClick, modifier = modifier) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(width = 72.dp, height = 132.dp)
                .previewChoiceRing(selected, c, shape)
                .padding(if (selected) 2.2.dp else 0.6.dp)
                .clip(shape)
                .background(if (darkPreview) Color(0xFF0A0C10) else Color(0xFFF2F2F7))
        ) {
            val bar = if (darkPreview) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.85f) else Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.75f)
            val card = if (darkPreview) Color(0xFF1C1C1E) else Color.White
            val glass = if (darkPreview) Color(0xCC505054) else Color(0xE6FFFFFF)
            PreviewMockBit(0.12f, 0.10f, 0.46f, 0.05f, bar, 3.dp)
            PreviewMockBit(0.09f, 0.24f, 0.82f, 0.25f, card, 8.dp)
            PreviewMockBit(0.09f, 0.54f, 0.82f, 0.17f, card, 8.dp)
            PreviewMockBit(0.09f, 0.84f, 0.62f, 0.10f, glass, 10.dp)
            PreviewMockBit(0.74f, 0.84f, 0.17f, 0.10f, c.tint.copy(alpha = 0.85f), 10.dp)
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun BoxScope.PreviewMockBit(
    x: Float,
    y: Float,
    w: Float,
    h: Float,
    color: Color,
    radius: Dp
) {
    Box(
        Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .drawBehind {
                drawRoundRect(
                    color = color,
                    topLeft = Offset(size.width * x, size.height * y),
                    size = Size(size.width * w, size.height * h),
                    cornerRadius = CornerRadius(radius.toPx())
                )
            }
    )
}

private val MaterialPreviewGradient = Brush.linearGradient(
    0f to Color(0xFFFF8D28),
    0.35f to Color(0xFFFF2D55),
    0.68f to Color(0xFF6155F5),
    1f to Color(0xFF00C3D0)
)

@Composable
private fun MaterialPreviewCard(
    kind: GlassMaterial,
    label: String,
    selected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp)
    val tinted = kind == GlassMaterial.TINTED
    PreviewChoiceBox(label = label, selected = selected, onClick = onClick, modifier = modifier) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(width = 132.dp, height = 96.dp)
                .previewChoiceRing(selected, c, shape)
                .padding(if (selected) 2.2.dp else 0.6.dp)
                .clip(shape)
                .background(MaterialPreviewGradient)
                .drawBehind {
                    val stripe = 5.dp.toPx()
                    val gap = 13.dp.toPx()
                    var x = 0f
                    while (x < size.width) {
                        drawRect(
                            color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.28f),
                            topLeft = Offset(x, 0f),
                            size = Size(stripe, size.height)
                        )
                        x += gap
                    }
                }
        ) {
            val capsule = GlassShapes.capsule
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.BottomCenter)
                    .padding(10.dp)
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(32.dp)
                    .shadow(4.dp, capsule, clip = false)
                    .clip(capsule)
                    .background(if (tinted) Color(0xD1FFFFFF) else Color(0x28FFFFFF))
                    .glassHighlight(capsule, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.95f))
                    .padding(horizontal = 8.dp),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceAround,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                val ink = if (tinted) Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.72f) else Color.White
                Box(Modifier.size(12.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp)).background(ink))
                Box(Modifier.size(12.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(ink))
                Box(Modifier.size(12.dp).clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)).background(ink))
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun WallpaperPreviewCard(
    wallpaper: GlassWallpaper,
    isDark: Boolean,
    palette: LiquidPalette,
    selected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
    val accent = palette.getAccent(isDark)
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = 48.dp)
            .clip(shape)
            .selectable(
                selected = selected,
                interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                indication = null,
                role = Role.RadioButton,
                onClick = onClick
            )
            .semantics {
                contentDescription = wallpaper.persianName
            },
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(68.dp)
                .previewChoiceRing(selected, c, shape)
                .padding(if (selected) 2.dp else 0.6.dp)
                .clip(shape)
                .drawBehind {
                    drawGlassWallpaper(wallpaper, isDark, accent)
                }
        ) {
            // Mini frosted glass pill inside wallpaper preview
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.Center)
                    .fillMaxWidth(0.72f)
                    .height(22.dp)
                    .clip(GlassShapes.capsule)
                    .background(
                        if (wallpaper.isDarkBackdrop(isDark)) {
                            Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.18f)
                        } else {
                            Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.72f)
                        }
                    )
                    .glassHighlight(GlassShapes.capsule, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.75f))
            )
            if (selected) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                        .padding(5.dp)
                        .size(18.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(c.tint),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Filled.Check,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = onColorFor(c.tint),
                        modifier = Modifier.size(12.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
        Text(
            text = wallpaper.persianName,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
            fontWeight = if (selected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium,
            color = if (selected) c.tint else c.label,
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun GroupedToggleRow(
    title: String,
    subtitle: String,
    checked: Boolean,
    onCheckedChange: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    enabled: Boolean = true
) {
    Row(
        modifier = modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .defaultMinSize(minHeight = 60.dp)
            .clickable(enabled = enabled) { onCheckedChange(!checked) }
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)
        ) {
            Text(
                text = title,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                color = if (enabled) LiquidTheme.textPrimary else LiquidTheme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = 0.55f)
            )
            Text(
                text = subtitle,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
                color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary.copy(alpha = if (enabled) 0.90f else 0.50f)
            )
        }
        LiquidSwitch(
            checked = checked,
            onCheckedChange = onCheckedChange,
            enabled = enabled
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun GlassFooterNote(text: String) {
    Text(
        text = text,
        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall,
        color = LiquidTheme.textSecondary,
        modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
    )
}

@Composable
private fun PaletteCard(
    palette: LiquidPalette,
    isSelected: Boolean,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    val c = Glass.colors
    val shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp)
    Surface(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .height(72.dp)
            .testTag("palette_card_${palette.id.lowercase()}")
            .semantics {
                role = Role.RadioButton
                selected = isSelected
                contentDescription = palette.persianName
            }
            .then(
                if (isSelected) {
                    Modifier.border(2.2.dp, palette.dayAccent, shape)
                } else {
                    Modifier.glassRim(shape, c, 0.8.dp)
                }
            )
            .liquidTapEffect { onClick() },
        shape = shape,
        color = Color.Transparent
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(palette.getCardGradient())
                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.20f))
                .glassHighlight(shape, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.45f), 1.dp)
                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(16.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(palette.dayAccent)
                            .border(1.dp, Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.7f), CircleShape)
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = palette.persianName,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = Color.White,
                        maxLines = 1,
                        overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis
                    )
                }

                if (isSelected) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(24.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(Color.White),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Filled.Check,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = palette.gradientStart,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: java/com/example/ui/screens/WorkoutsScreen.kt =====
package com.example.ui.screens

import androidx.compose.animation.core.Animatable
import androidx.compose.foundation.background
import androidx.compose.foundation.clickable
import androidx.compose.foundation.gestures.detectHorizontalDragGestures
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.LazyColumn
import androidx.compose.foundation.lazy.items
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.CircleShape
import androidx.compose.foundation.shape.RoundedCornerShape
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.automirrored.filled.ArrowBack
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.*
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.rememberSaveable
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.draw.clip
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Brush
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import androidx.compose.ui.input.pointer.pointerInput
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalDensity
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.testTag
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.IntOffset
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import kotlin.math.roundToInt
import com.example.data.local.*
import com.example.ui.components.*
import com.example.ui.theme.*
import com.example.util.JalaliCalendar
import com.example.util.PersianNumberHelper
import com.example.util.PlanShareHelper
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

@Composable
fun WorkoutsScreen(
    allPlans: List<WorkoutPlan>,
    allHistories: List<WorkoutHistory>,
    allSkips: List<WorkoutPlanSkip>,
    onStartWorkout: (WorkoutPlan) -> Unit,
    onEditPlan: (WorkoutPlan?) -> Unit,
    onDuplicatePlan: (Long) -> Unit,
    onDeletePlan: (Long) -> Unit,
    onReschedulePlan: (Long, String, String?) -> Unit,
    onUndoSkip: (Long, String) -> Unit,
    onDeleteHistory: (Long) -> Unit,
    onImportPlanJson: (String) -> Unit,
    onGetPlanExercises: suspend (Long) -> List<WorkoutExercise>,
    onDuplicateAllPlans: () -> Unit,
    isWorkoutActive: Boolean = false,
    activeWorkoutPlanId: Long? = null,
    activeWorkoutPlanName: String = "",
    onResumeActiveWorkout: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    var selectedTab by rememberSaveable { mutableIntStateOf(0) } // 0: برنامه‌ها, 1: تاریخچه
    var searchQuery by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var planToReschedule by remember { mutableStateOf<WorkoutPlan?>(null) }
    var planToDelete by remember { mutableStateOf<WorkoutPlan?>(null) }
    var showImportDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var importJsonText by remember { mutableStateOf("") }

    val context = LocalContext.current
    val coroutineScope = rememberCoroutineScope()
    val todayGregorian = remember { JalaliCalendar.getTodayGregorian() }
    val todayWeekday = remember { JalaliCalendar.getTodayWeekdayPersian() }

    val filteredPlans = remember(allPlans, searchQuery) {
        if (searchQuery.isBlank()) allPlans
        else allPlans.filter { it.name.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true) || it.notes.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true) }
    }

    val (todayPlans, otherPlans) = remember(filteredPlans, todayWeekday, allSkips, todayGregorian) {
        val postponedToToday = allSkips.filter { it.newDate == todayGregorian }.map { it.planId }
        val skippedToday = allSkips.filter {
            (it.originalDate == todayGregorian && it.newDate == null) ||
                (it.originalDate == todayGregorian && it.newDate != todayGregorian)
        }.map { it.planId }

        filteredPlans.partition { plan ->
            (plan.scheduledDay.contains(todayWeekday) || postponedToToday.contains(plan.id)) && !skippedToday.contains(plan.id)
        }
    }

    val groupedHistories = remember(allHistories, searchQuery) {
        val list = if (searchQuery.isBlank()) allHistories
        else allHistories.filter { it.planName.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true) || it.exerciseName.contains(searchQuery, ignoreCase = true) }
        list.groupBy { it.date }
    }

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .testTag("workouts_screen"),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(bottom = com.example.ui.components.LocalLiquidBottomClearance.current + 20.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        // 1. Inline Liquid Top Bar with 44dp Circular Glass Add Button
        item {
            LiquidTopBar(
                title = "برنامه‌های تمرینی",
                navigationIcon = {
                    LiquidCircleButton(
                        icon = Icons.Filled.FileDownload,
                        contentDescription = "ایمپورت برنامه",
                        onClick = { showImportDialog = true }
                    )
                },
                actions = {
                    if (allPlans.isNotEmpty()) {
                        LiquidCircleButton(
                            icon = Icons.Filled.ContentCopy,
                            contentDescription = "کپی متن تمامی برنامه‌ها",
                            onClick = {
                                coroutineScope.launch {
                                    val fullText = StringBuilder()
                                    fullText.append("📋 برنامه‌های تمرینی GYM PRO\n\n")
                                    allPlans.forEach { plan ->
                                        val exercises = onGetPlanExercises(plan.id)
                                        fullText.append(PlanShareHelper.formatPlanAsShareableText(plan, exercises))
                                        fullText.append("\n-------------------\n\n")
                                    }
                                    PlanShareHelper.copyToClipboard(context, "برنامه‌های تمرینی", fullText.toString())
                                    Toast.makeText(context, "متن تمامی برنامه‌ها در حافظه کپی شد (آماده ارسال به دیگران).", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                }
                            }
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                    }
                    LiquidCircleButton(
                        icon = Icons.Filled.Add,
                        contentDescription = "برنامه جدید",
                        onClick = { onEditPlan(null) }
                    )
                }
            )
        }

        // 2. Pill Search Field
        item {
            Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                LiquidSearchField(
                    value = searchQuery,
                    onValueChange = { searchQuery = it },
                    placeholder = if (selectedTab == 0) "جستجو در نام یا روزهای برنامه‌ها..." else "جستجو در سوابق تمرینات..."
                )
            }
        }

        // 3. Segmented Control: «برنامه‌ها / تاریخچه»
        item {
            Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                LiquidSegmentedControl(
                    items = listOf("برنامه‌های تمرینی", "سوابق و تاریخچه"),
                    selectedIndex = selectedTab,
                    onSelectIndex = { selectedTab = it }
                )
            }
        }

        if (selectedTab == 0) {
            if (isWorkoutActive) {
                item(key = "active_workout_resume_banner") {
                    Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                        ContentRow(
                            title = activeWorkoutPlanName.ifBlank { "تمرین در حال اجرا" },
                            subtitle = "بازگشت به جلسه فعال بدون از دست رفتن ست‌ها",
                            icon = Icons.Filled.PlayArrow,
                            badgeText = "ادامه تمرین",
                            gradient = LiquidTheme.cardGradient,
                            onClick = onResumeActiveWorkout
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // Plans Tab
            if (filteredPlans.isEmpty()) {
                item {
                    EmptyStateCard(
                        icon = Icons.Filled.FitnessCenter,
                        title = if (searchQuery.isNotBlank()) "برنامه‌ای با این مشخصات یافت نشد" else "هنوز برنامه‌ای تعریف نشده است",
                        description = if (searchQuery.isNotBlank()) "عبارت جستجو را تغییر دهید" else "یک برنامه اختصاصی جدید بسازید یا برنامه دوستانتان را ایمپورت کنید.",
                        actionButtonText = if (searchQuery.isBlank()) "ساخت برنامه جدید" else null,
                        onActionClick = { onEditPlan(null) }
                    )
                }
            } else {
                // Section: Today's plans
                if (todayPlans.isNotEmpty()) {
                    item {
                        LiquidSectionHeader(title = "نوبت تمرین امروز ($todayWeekday)")
                    }

                    items(todayPlans, key = { "today_${it.id}" }) { plan ->
                        val isRunning = isWorkoutActive && plan.id == activeWorkoutPlanId
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            SwipeablePlanCard(
                                onDelete = { planToDelete = plan },
                                onEdit = { onEditPlan(plan) },
                                onCopyText = {
                                    coroutineScope.launch {
                                        val exercises = onGetPlanExercises(plan.id)
                                        val text = PlanShareHelper.formatPlanAsShareableText(plan, exercises)
                                        PlanShareHelper.copyToClipboard(context, "برنامه ${plan.name}", text)
                                        Toast.makeText(context, "متن برنامه «${plan.name}» کپی شد (آماده ارسال به دیگران).", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                    }
                                }
                            ) {
                                ContentRow(
                                    title = plan.name,
                                    subtitle = if (isRunning) "در حال اجرا • لمس برای ادامه ست‌ها" else "برنامه امروز • ${plan.scheduledDay}",
                                    icon = Icons.Filled.PlayArrow,
                                    badgeText = if (isRunning) "در حال اجرا" else "شروع نوبت",
                                    gradient = LiquidTheme.cardGradient,
                                    onClick = { if (isRunning) onResumeActiveWorkout() else onStartWorkout(plan) }
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Section: Other plans
                if (otherPlans.isNotEmpty()) {
                    item {
                        LiquidSectionHeader(title = if (todayPlans.isNotEmpty()) "سایر برنامه‌ها" else "تمامی برنامه‌ها")
                    }

                    items(otherPlans, key = { "other_${it.id}" }) { plan ->
                        val isRunning = isWorkoutActive && plan.id == activeWorkoutPlanId
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            SwipeablePlanCard(
                                onDelete = { planToDelete = plan },
                                onEdit = { onEditPlan(plan) },
                                onCopyText = {
                                    coroutineScope.launch {
                                        val exercises = onGetPlanExercises(plan.id)
                                        val text = PlanShareHelper.formatPlanAsShareableText(plan, exercises)
                                        PlanShareHelper.copyToClipboard(context, "برنامه ${plan.name}", text)
                                        Toast.makeText(context, "متن برنامه «${plan.name}» کپی شد (آماده ارسال به دیگران).", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                    }
                                }
                            ) {
                                ContentRow(
                                    title = plan.name,
                                    subtitle = if (isRunning) "در حال اجرا • لمس برای ادامه ست‌ها" else "روزهای اجرا: ${plan.scheduledDay}",
                                    icon = if (isRunning) Icons.Filled.PlayArrow else Icons.Filled.FitnessCenter,
                                    badgeText = if (isRunning) "در حال اجرا" else null,
                                    gradient = LiquidTheme.cardGradient,
                                    onClick = { if (isRunning) onResumeActiveWorkout() else onStartWorkout(plan) }
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        } else {
            // History Tab
            if (groupedHistories.isEmpty()) {
                item {
                    EmptyStateCard(
                        icon = Icons.Filled.History,
                        title = "هنوز سابقه‌ای ثبت نشده است",
                        description = "پس از به پایان رساندن جلسات تمرینی، خلاصه ست‌ها و زمان در این بخش ثبت می‌شود."
                    )
                }
            } else {
                groupedHistories.forEach { (dateStr, histories) ->
                    val jDate = JalaliCalendar.formatGregorianToPersian(dateStr)

                    item(key = "hdr_$dateStr") {
                        LiquidSectionHeader(title = jDate)
                    }

                    item(key = "card_$dateStr") {
                        Box(modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp)) {
                            SolidGroupedCard {
                                histories.forEachIndexed { index, history ->
                                    val subtitle = "وزن: ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(history.weight)} کیلوگرم • ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(history.sets)} ست × ${PersianNumberHelper.toPersianDigits(history.reps)} تکرار"

                                    GroupedRow(
                                        title = history.exerciseName,
                                        subtitle = subtitle,
                                        icon = Icons.Filled.CheckCircle,
                                        iconTint = SemanticSuccess,
